| \#\# \*\*Beef Ribs\*\*    |

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*BBQ Oven Baked Ribs\*\*By \[Soul Food Cooking\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wkYRZhVcfT0) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 4.5 lb Beef ribs &#10;  - Canola oil&#10;  - 1/2 Tbsp Onion powder&#10;  - 1/2 Tbsp Garlic powder&#10;  - 1/2 Tbsp Black pepper&#10;  - 1/2 Tbsp Salt&#10;  - Barbecue sauce     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Preheat the oven to 385 degrees.&#10;2.  Brush ribs with canola oil.&#10;3.  Prepare seasoning mixture by adding  onion powder, garlic powder, black pepper, and salt to a small bowl.  Season ribs generously with this seasoning mixture.&#10;4.  Wrap ribs with aluminum foil and place into the oven for 3.5 hours.&#10;5.  Remove ribs and brush with barbecue sauce. &#10;6.  Change the oven setting to broil and cook ribs for an additional 2 minutes.&#10;7.  Rebrush with more barbecue sauce per preference.    |

  

|  |
| :-: |
| \#\# \*\*Cubed Beef\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Beef Stew\*\*By \[Tatyana’s Everyday Food\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8p-f9DcVkgE) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Lb Beef tri tip, large cubes&#10;  - 3 Medium Carrots, diced&#10;  - 1 Large Onion, diced&#10;  - 3 Medium Sticks Celery, diced&#10;  - 4 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 10 Mushrooms, thinly slices&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Flour&#10;  - 1 Cup Red wine &#10;  - 1 Cup Tomato Sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Worcestershire sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Smoked Paprika&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 2 ½ Tsp of Italian herbs (Thyme, Oregano?)&#10;  - 1 ½ Tbsp Salt&#10;  - 7-8 Cracks Black pepper&#10;  - 4 Cups Beef broth&#10;  - 2 Cups Baby Potatoes, halved&#10;  - 1 Cup Frozen Sweet Peas&#10;  - Parsley, chopped.   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pan on medium-high heat with oil.&#10;2.  Add beef. Season generously with salt. Cook for 7-10 minutes until well browned and seared.&#10;3.  Transfer beef to large stew pot. &#10;4.  Deglaze pan with ½ cup water and then add to stew pot.&#10;5.  Add more oil to pan. Add carrots, onions, and celery. Saute for 4-6 minutes until caramelized.&#10;6.  Add garlic and cook for about 1 minute.&#10;7.  Add everything to stew pot. &#10;8.  To pan add 2 tbsp butter until melted. Then add mushrooms. Brown mushrooms for 4-5 minutes. Then season with salt. Add flour and toss with mushrooms for about 1 minute. Add everything to pot.&#10;9.  To pot, add the red wine, tomato sauce, Worcerstershire sauce, paprika, suga, italian herbs, salt, black peper, and beef broth.&#10;10. Cover pot and set to high heat until boiling. Lower heat and simmer for 1-1.5 hours.&#10;11. Add potatoes and peas. Cook for 20 minutes until potatoes are fork tender.&#10;12. Top with freshly chopped parsley. \*\*Notes\*\*       |

  

|  |
| :-: |
| \#\# \*\*Ground Beef\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Chili\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YdFjuglEAds\&t=8s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 3 lb Ground beef&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Ground pork&#10;  - 1 Onion, yellow, diced&#10;  - 6 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Tomato paste&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Oregano&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Chili powder&#10;  - 2 Tsp Garlic powder&#10;  - 2 Tsp Onion powder&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Cumin powder&#10;  - 2x 10oz cans of diced tomatoes (Rotel preferred). &#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Whole peeled tomatoes mashed&#10;  - Optional: Jalapeno or habanero pepper, bell pepper&#10;  - 3 Cups Chicken stock&#10;  - 1 Can Beans (Kidney beans)&#10;  - 2 Bay leaves&#10;  - Optional: Fish sauce, Sugar   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  On high heat, add oil to the dutch oven. Add ground beef and let it brown on one side.&#10;2.  Mix and let brown more&#10;3.  Add onions and garlic. Mix and cook until onions are a little soft.&#10;4.  Season with salt and pepper.&#10;5.  Add tomato paste and spices (oregano, chili powder, garlic powder, onion powder, cumin). Mix and cook to let spices bloom.&#10;6.  Add diced tomatoes. (Add peppers at this time if using in recipe).&#10;7.  Add chicken stock.&#10;8.  Add beans and mix.&#10;9.  Add bay leaves and let simmer for a bit.&#10;10. Add fish sauce and sugar to taste. \*\*Notes\*\*  - Can use \*\*flour\*\* to thicken chili.&#10;  - Can top with \*\*cheese\*\*, \*\*green onions\*\*, \*\*sour cream\*\*, and \*\*cilantro\*\*.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Cheeseburger and Fries\*\*By Junmac |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Ground beef&#10;  - Onion powder&#10;  - Garlic powder&#10;  - Black pepper, ground&#10;  - Hamburger buns&#10;  - Iceberg lettuce&#10;  - Onions, sliced&#10;  - Tomatoes, sliced&#10;  - \[Dill Pickles\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.jxgiuc6t6khe)&#10;  - American cheese singles\*\*For Sauce:\*\*  - 1 Tbsp Ketchup&#10;  - Sriracha, pre preference&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Kewpie Mayo&#10;  - 1/2 Tbsp Dijon Mustard&#10;  - 1 Tsp Onion powder&#10;  - 1 Tsp Garlic powder  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Prepare \[Dill Pickles\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.jxgiuc6t6khe) and \[Crispy Oven Baked French Fries\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.jebz806i2uem).&#10;2.  Season ground beef with onion powder, garlic powder, and black pepper per preference. Mix in with beef. Roll beef into balls/patties..&#10;3.  Prepare sauce.&#10;4.  Cook onions to preference.&#10;5.  Salt tomatoes.&#10;6.  Toast hamburger buns in the toaster oven or on a pan with butter/oil.&#10;7.  Heat pan on high heat. Add a hamburger ball and smash to preferred size. Season top with salt and pepper. Cook until browned. Flip, season, and cook the other side. Add cheese to top of the patty.&#10;8.  Assemble burgers with bun, sauce, patty, tomatoes, dill pickles, onions, and lettuce.&#10;9.  Serve with fries on the side. \*\*Notes\*\*       |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Holy Basil Stir Fry (Pad Kra Pao)\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fYhTo\_gb1G8\&t=29s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Ground beef&#10;  - 2-3 Thai chilis, minced or mashed&#10;  - 6 Cloves Garlic, minced or mashed&#10;  - 2 Fresno chilis, minced or mashed (mild red chilis)&#10;  - 1 Cup Holy basil or Thai basil or Italian basil&#10;  - 1 ½ Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - Optional: Bell peppers  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pan on high. Add beef once hot and brown. Cook until some searing/browning, then lower heat.&#10;2.  Add oil and add garlic and chilis to side of beef.. Saute. Increase heat.&#10;3.  Add ½ cup of water or unsalted stock.&#10;4.  Add fish sauce and sugar.&#10;5.  Stir fry until sauce reduced.&#10;6.  Add julienned bell peppers for color and crunch.&#10;7.  Turn off heat and add basil leaves.&#10;8.  Top with fried egg and serve with rice.&#10;9.  Top egg with a little bit of Prik Nam Pla \*\*Notes\*\*       |

  

|  |
| :-: |
| \#\# \*\*Oxtail\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Sinigang incomplete\*\*By Otakoyakisoba |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Pork &#10;  - Radish&#10;  - Taro&#10;  - Long beans&#10;  - Chili&#10;  - Tomato&#10;  - Onions&#10;  - Tamarind&#10;  - Fish sauce    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cook pork with water until boiling, then toss water.&#10;2.  Rinse/wash meat.&#10;3.  In a pot, boil beef on high heat while covered with salt and pepper for 1-3 hours to tenderize. Once you can poke through with a fork, cook for another 30 minutes.&#10;4.  In a separate pot, heat oil on medium. Add garlic and onions. Add Achuete package when hot.  Add some of the beef broth from the pot. &#10;5.  Once boiling, add eggplant and green beans. Cook for 5-10 minutes. &#10;6.  Add peanut butter and mix. Can add more per preference of thickness and taste.&#10;7.  Add meat to pot.&#10;8.  Add bok choy at the end. Can add salt and pepper to taste.&#10;9.  Serve with shrimp paste (Bagoong) \*\*Notes\*\*Could |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Kare Kare\*\*By Mama Tessie |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Beef shank, chopped into cubes&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Oxtail (More expensive)&#10;  - Green beans, Long beans, or String beans, sliced into 2-3 inch pieces&#10;  - Bok choy, Napa cabbage,  or Romaine lettuce&#10;  - 1 Egg plant, sliced per preference&#10;  - 1-2 Garlic clove, minced&#10;  - ¼ Onion, chopped&#10;  - 2-5 Tbsp Peanut butter, creamy&#10;  - ½  Package Achuete/Annatto powder    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cook beef with water until boiling, then toss water.&#10;2.  Rinse/wash meat.&#10;3.  In a pot, boil beef on low-medium heat while covered with salt and pepper for 1-3 hours to tenderize. Once you can poke through with a fork, cook for another 30 minutes.&#10;4.  In a separate pot, heat oil on medium. Add garlic and onions. Add Achuete package when hot.  Add some of the beef broth from the pot. &#10;5.  Once boiling, add eggplant and green beans. Cook for 5-10 minutes. &#10;6.  Add peanut butter and mix. Can add more per preference of thickness and taste.&#10;7.  Add meat to pot.&#10;8.  Add bok choy at the end. Can add salt and pepper to taste.&#10;9.  Serve with shrimp paste (Bagoong)    |

  

|  |
| :-: |
| \#\# \*\*Thinly Sliced Beef\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Beef Steak\*\*By Mama Tessie |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Rib eye beef, sliced  into bite sized pieces&#10;  - Orchid’s Korean BBQ Sauce&#10;  - 1 Onion, thinly chopped&#10;  - 1-2 Green onion stalks, thinly chopped&#10;  - Pinch garlic salt    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Marinate beef with Orchid’s Korean BBQ Sauce for 1-2 days&#10;2.  Heat pan on medium with olive oil.&#10;3.  Cook meat until brown.&#10;4.  Saute onions in a separate pan with olive oil.&#10;5.  Combine onions and meat.&#10;6.  Add green onions and cook for 2o seconds.&#10;7.  Serve with white rice. \*\*Notes\*\*If not marinating, cook meat first, then add sauce and cook. Alternative to Orchid’s Korean BBQ Sauce:  - 1 Part soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Part vinegar&#10;  - 1 Part fish sauce&#10;  - 1 Part sugar  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Beef Tapa\*\*By \[RV Manabat\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UWgJuCL\_NU4) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 kg Beef (tenderloin or sirloin), thinly  sliced\*\*For marinade:\*\*  - ½-1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - ½ Tsp salt&#10;  - 1 Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - 2 Garlic heads, minced&#10;  - ½ Cup + 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Calamansi or Lemon juice&#10;  - ½ Cup cola, Apple juice, or Pineapple juice       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Make marinade by mixing all marinade ingredients.&#10;2.  Marinade beef in marinade for 1 day in the refrigerator or cook right away.&#10;3.  Cook pork on medium heat until fully cooked and slightly browned.&#10;4.  Serve with rice. \*\*Notes\*\*\*\*For Tapsilog\*\* (Tapa breakfast meal), serve with:  - \[Garlic Fried Rice\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.rex0atktdww1)&#10;  - \[Sukang Sawsawan (Spiced Vinegar)\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.7q4tg0hf75ba)&#10;  - Fried egg&#10;  - Tomatoes (Roma preferred), chopped into small pieces      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Garlic Pepper Beef\*\*By \[Ninong Ry\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=w-pajKoxCaY) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Beef, thinly sliced&#10;  - 5 Garlic cloves, thinly sliced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp black pepper&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - 2 Tbsp flour&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Garlic powder   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat oil on medium heat.&#10;2.  Add garlic until slightly browned, then add beef.&#10;3.  Add black pepper and soy sauce. Cook until beef is cooked, and then set aside.&#10;4.  Add butter to the pan.&#10;5.  Add flour until some browning.&#10;6.  Optional to add a beef cube or beef bouillon.&#10;7.  Add 3 Tbsp water.&#10;8.  Add garlic powder.&#10;9.  Top off with a few cracks of black pepper.         |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Gochujang Stew (Jjiggae)\*\*By \[Future Neighbor\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9UfuP2ssg3s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Beef, thinly sliced&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Pork&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Vegetable oil&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Red chili pepper paste (Gochujang)&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Red chili pepper flakes (Gochugaru)&#10;  - 1 Potato, thinly chopped&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Garlic, minced&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - ½ Onion, thinly chopped&#10;  - Mushrooms, thinly chopped&#10;  - 2 Green chili peppers or 1 jalapeno, thinly chopped&#10;  - 2 Green onion stalks, thinly chopped&#10;  - Black pepper   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cook the pan/wok on low heat with vegetable oil and sesame oil.&#10;2.  Add Gochujang .&#10;3.  Once bubbling, add Gochugaru and then 2.5 cups of water immediately.&#10;4.  Raise to medium-high heat and add potatoes and beef.&#10;5.  Add garlic, soy sauce, and fish sauce. Boil for 5 minutes until fish sauce is cooked away and potatoes are soft.&#10;6.  Add onions, mushrooms, and green chili peppers. Boil and stir for 5-10 minutes.&#10;7.  Add green onions.&#10;8.  Add 8 cracks of black pepper.&#10;9.  Can add water if too salty.&#10;10. Serve with white rice.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Gyudon (Beef Rice Bowl)\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=56ajLLO09wU) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Beef, thinly sliced (Beef chuck or rib eye)&#10;  - 1 Onion, sliced&#10;  - ½ Cup Dashi stock &#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: 1 Tsp Hondashi &#10;  - ¼ Cup Dry sake &#10;  - 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tsp Ginger, minced&#10;  - 2 Stalk Green onion, chopped&#10;  - Shichimi togarashi (Japanese chili powder)&#10;  - 2 Fried eggs or \[Poached Eggs\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.ofwrl4p2ietj)    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To pan, add dashi stock, sake, soy sauce, sugar, and onions. Bring to a simmer and cook until onions are tender.&#10;2.  Add beef and cook until water is reduced to 1/3. About 5 minutes.&#10;3.  Add ginger to the pan and mix.&#10;4.  Adjust with sugar or soy sauce per preference.&#10;5.  Serve over rice with eggs and shichimi togarashi. \*\*Notes\*\*Could be served with beni-shoga (pickled ginger).    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Honey Beef\*\*By \[Ninong Ry\](https://youtu.be/w-pajKoxCaY?si=VD1hzTsglcKhfk5D\&t=359) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of beef \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Beef, thinly sliced&#10;  - 1 Onion, thinly sliced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Flour&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Oyster sauce or Hoisin sauce.&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Honey  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat oil on medium heat.&#10;2.  Add beef and soy sauce. Cook until brown.&#10;3.  Add onions. Cook and then set aside.&#10;4.  Add butter to the pan.&#10;5.  Once butter is melted, add flour. Mix.&#10;6.  Add 3 Tbsp water.&#10;7.  Add oyster sauce or hoisin sauce.&#10;8.  Add honey.&#10;9.  Turn off heat and add 1 Tbsp butter.&#10;10. Stir to melt butter. Emulsify butter.&#10;11. Serve right away before the sauce becomes runny.         |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Mongolian Beef\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=X9E6HPZurmE\&t=273s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Beef (flank steak, new york), thinly sliced&#10;  - 0.75 Onion (red or white), sliced into ¼ inch strips&#10;  - 4-5 Green onions, sliced into 2 inch pieces&#10;  - 4-5 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - 4 Chili peppers, dried&#10;  - Optional: 1-2 Red/Green bell peppers for color, sliced\*\*For Marinade:\*\*  - 2 Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Cornstarch&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Water&#10;  - 2 Tsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 2 Tsp Light soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tsp Dark soy sauce&#10;  - ¼ Tsp White pepper\*\*For Sauce:\*\*  - 2 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Light soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Dark soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Tsp Chili sauce, adjusted to preference&#10;  - 2 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Cooking wine  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Wash beef in a bowl with water to rinse out myoglobin. Drain and set aside.&#10;2.  Prepare Marinade by mixing all ingredients in a mixing bowl.&#10;3.  Squeeze out extra water from beef, then mix beef into Marinade until dry.&#10;4.  Prepare vegetables at this time.&#10;5.  Prepare Sauce by mixing ingredients into a bowl.&#10;6.  Add 1-2 Tbsp of oil to beef once done marinating and mix to seal in marinade. Add 1.5 Tbsp cornstarch to beef and mix.&#10;7.  Heat pan on high until hot. Add oil and heat on high, enough oil to fry beef.&#10;8.  Add beef and cook for 1.5 minutes. Should be slightly crispy. Remove beef and let oil drain off. The oil can be saved and used to cook other meals.&#10;9.  Heat pan with  some oil on medium.. Add garlic and chili peppers. Then add onions and white portion of green onions. Then add bell peppers. Stir fry until cooked, about 1-1.5 minutes. &#10;10. Add beef. After 20 seconds, add the Sauce and green portion of green onions. Cook for about 30 seconds.&#10;11.  Add 1 Tbsp sesame oil and cook for 20 seconds.&#10;12. Serve with rice. \*\*Notes\*\*Cut beef against the grain for tenderness.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Spicy Beef Bulgogi\*\*By \[Maangchi\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mQzjhjSxMUc\&t=87s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 lb Beef (flank steak, new york), thinly sliced&#10;  - 4 Green onion stalks, sliced into 2-3 inch pieces&#10;  - 1 Oyster mushroom, sliced\*\*For Sauce:\*\*  - 6 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - ¼ Cup Gochugaru (Korean chili flakes)&#10;  - ¼ Cup Rice Syrup or Honey&#10;  - ¼ Cup Gochujang (Korean chili paste)&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Cup water&#10;  - ½ Tsp Black pepper  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Prepare Sauce by mixing ingredients into a bowl.&#10;2.  Add beef to marinade and mix. Can marinade or cook right away.&#10;3.  Heat pan on medium-high with oil until hot.&#10;4.  Cook green onions and mushrooms for 1 minute.&#10;5.  Add beef with marinade and cook on medium.&#10;6.  Once cooked, remove meat and vegetables. Add rice and sliced kimchi to prepare kimchi fried rice. \*\*Notes\*\*     |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Thai Mint Beef\*\*Modified recipe by \[Thai Girl in the Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iJo0\_RPdwV8) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Beef (flank steak, new york), thinly sliced&#10;  - 20-25 Mint leaves&#10;  - 2-3 Shallot, thinly chopped &#10;  - 2-3 Stalk Green onions, minced&#10;  - ½ Cup Cilantro&#10;  - 1-2 Tbsp Ground toasted rice\*\*For Marinade:\*\*  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Oyster Sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Light Soy sauce&#10;  - Black pepper&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Water\*\*For Dressing:\*\*  - 1 Tbsp  Fish sauce&#10;  - 1-2 Tbsp water&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Lime juice &#10;  - ½ Tsp sugar&#10;  - Chili flakes to taste   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Prepare marinade by mixing ingredients into a bowl. Marinade beef in the refrigerator for at least 1 hour.&#10;2.  Prepare dressing by mixing ingredients in a bowl.&#10;3.  Heat pan on medium with oil until hot.&#10;4.  Cook beef on the pan or grill until medium to medium-rare.&#10;5.  Once beef is cooked add shallots and green onion. Cook for 1-2 minutes.&#10;6.  Turn off heat, add mint and cilantro and mix.&#10;7.  Add dressing ingredients and toss.&#10;8.  Before serving, add ground toasted rice.&#10;9.  Serve with rice. \*\*Notes\*\*To make ground toasted rice, heat raw rice in a pan until golden brown. Can add a lime leaf when toasting. Then grind in a mortar and pestle.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Tomato Beef Stir Fry\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://youtu.be/6C9aYezLqbQ?si=QgAjcyIxyeIUVZMN) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Lb Beef (flank steak, new york), thinly sliced&#10;  - 5 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - ¼ Cup Shallots, thinly sliced&#10;  - 1 Nub Ginger, minced&#10;  - 1 Lb Roma tomatoes, sliced into wedges (3-4 tomatoes)&#10;  - 2 Stalk Green onions, sliced\*\*For Marinade:\*\*  - 1 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Tsp Garlic powder&#10;  - ½ Tsp Ginger powder&#10;  - ½ Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Water\*\*For Sauce:\*\*  - ¼  Cup Ketchup&#10;  - 2 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Cornstarch&#10;  - 2 Tbsp water | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Dissolve baking soda in water and add to beef. Mix, then add the rest of the marinade ingredients.&#10;2.  Prepare sauce.&#10;3.  If serving with eggs, can cook eggs first in oil, then set aside.&#10;4.  On high heat with oil, add beef and cook until 90% done. Set side.&#10;5.  On medium-high heat, and more oil and add garlic, ginger, and shallots until garlic starts to turn golden.&#10;6.  Add tomatoes and toss just until the exterior starts to soften. \\\~30 seconds.&#10;7.  Turn heat to high and add beef and whites of green onions. &#10;8.  Add sauce and toss until the sauce is bubbling. \\\~30 seconds.&#10;9.  Turn off the heat and stir in green onions.&#10;10. Serve with rice and eggs. \*\*Notes\*\*Cut beef against the grain for tenderness.    |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Reverse Sear Steak\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pO8TUuSv7HA) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Steak&#10;  - Salt and pepper     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Can salt steak the night before and place onto drying rack. Salt a lot. You want surface of steak to be as dry as possible before cooking. Can put in fridge for 1-7 days.&#10;2.  Season with pepper.&#10;3.  Cook in oven at 250 degrees F. May take 2+ hours.&#10;4.  Super hot pan. Add neutral high temp oil.&#10;5.  Add steak to pan. Sear each side for a couple of minutes. Until brown. \*\*Notes\*\*Good for 1.5+ inch steaks.Can serve with \[Chimichurri Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.fnfdwbq3ape3).  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Steak\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Steak&#10;  - Salt and pepper&#10;  - Butter&#10;  - Garlic or Garlic powder&#10;  - Optional: Thyme or rosemary    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Salt the steak generously and place onto a drying rack.  Place in the refrigerator for 1-3 days. Surface of steak should be as dry as possible.&#10;2.  Take the steak out of the refrigerator for 20 minutes.&#10;3.  Heat pan on high then add olive oil. Thicker steaks do not require as high heat to avoid burning. Get a nice sizzle.&#10;4.  Add steaks to pan to sear each side and flipping every minute or so.&#10;5.  Lower to medium heat and add 2 nobs of butter and 4 garlic cloves. Could add thyme.&#10;6.  Use a spoon to baste steak with butte and oil.&#10;7.  Cook until desired internal temperature is reached. Take steak off of pan 5-10 degrees early. \|  \|  \|&#10;\| :-: \| :-: \|&#10;\| Rare \|  120 F \|&#10;\| Medium rare to Medium \| 125-135 F \|&#10;\| Medium well \| 140-145 F \|&#10;1\.  Let rest before cutting. Season with pepper. \*\*Notes\*\*Strip steak is good but more costly.Top sirloin is great value.T Bone good but expensive. Comes from loin and tenderloin.Rib eye is marbled and tasty, juicy. Comes from the loin.New york steak, aka top loin. Cut from short loin of cow. Same muscle as ribeye but further back.Good steak has fat marbled into meat.Higher fat content, might taste better the higher cooked. Fat can render more. Can serve with \[Chimichurri Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.fnfdwbq3ape3).  |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Pan-Fried Chicken Breast\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P7bHbYkQ6RU\&t=24s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Chicken thighs or breasts, cut into 1 inch pieces&#10;  -       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a bowl, add 2 cups of water and 1 tsp salt.&#10;2.  Add chicken to fully submerge. Brine. For 30-60 minutes.&#10;3.  Cut chicken into 3 equal pieces against the grain. Then cut 1 inch thick chicken cutlets. Then pound chicken.&#10;4.  Make marinade: 2 Tbsp oyster sauce, 2 tsp light soy sauce, 1 Tbsp cooking wine, 1 Tsp sugar, ½ Tsp salt, ¼ Tsp white pepper, 2 Tbsp cornstarch, 1 Tbsp water, ½ oz grated ginger. Add meat and mix well.&#10;5.  ½ onion sliced into thin strips (top to root), ½ red onon. 1 Tbsp oyster sauce, 2 Tsp Light soy sauce, 2 Tsp sugar, 1 Tsp chicken bouillon powder, 1 Tsp cornstarch, 3 tbsp water,1 Tbsp cooking wine.&#10;6.  On plate place 2-3 tbsp cornstarch, dredge chicken in it. &#10;7.  Heat oil on high. Lay cutlets one at a time. Wait until golden brown before flipping. Fry for another minute.&#10;8.  Add onions and spread them out. Add 1 tsp cooking wine and cover for 30 seconds.  Add sauce over chicken. Agitate and move chicken around. Cook each side low to medium heat for 3 minutes each. 165 degrees in center, can stop around 160.&#10;9.  Add 1 tsp sesame oil.  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Arroz Caldo\*\*By Mama Tessie |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Chicken thighs or breasts, cut into 1 inch pieces&#10;  - 4-6 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - 1-2 Ginger nubs, minced&#10;  - ¼  Onion, thinly chopped&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Fish sauce (Patis)&#10;  - 1 Cup rice, uncooked&#10;  - 1 Green onion stalk, thinly chopped&#10;  - Lemon or Lemon juice    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat the pot on medium heat and add olive oil to coat the bottom of the pot.&#10;2.  Add garlic and ginger until brown.&#10;3.  Add onions and saute.&#10;4.  Add chicken and saute to brown.&#10;5.  Add fish sauce and saute.&#10;6.  Cover pot to cook for a few minutes.&#10;7.  Add 6 cups of water.&#10;8.  Add rice and continue to cook on medium heat until rice is fully cooked, at least 10 minutes. Can cover the pot and monitor to ensure it does not overflow. Rice should be soft.&#10;9.  Add pepper and fish sauce to taste.&#10;10. Can add green onions or serve on the side. Serve with lemon or lemon juice on the side.  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Black Pepper Chicken\*\*By \[Tess Cooks 4u\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=h\_3uPWEnOZ0\&t=5s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Lb Chicken Thighs or Breast, sliced into small pieces (1 Costco pack)&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Chili vinegar&#10;      &#10;      - 2 Chili peppers&#10;      - ½ Cup Vinegar&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Tsp Ground ginger&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Cornstarch &#10;  - 1-1 ½ Tbsp Black peppercorns, ground&#10;  - 2-3 stalks Celery, sliced&#10;  - 1 Onion, sliced     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add chili peppers and vinegar to the pot. Heat to boil and simmer for 15 seconds.&#10;2.  To chicken, add chili vinegar, soy sauce, ground ginger, and cornstarch. Mix and marinate for 30 minutes.&#10;3.  Pan on high heat with oil. Add chicken and stir fry for 5-7 minutes or until cooked. Remove chicken from the pan.&#10;4.  Then cook onion and celery. Stir fry then readd chicken and stir to combine.&#10;5.  Add 1 Tbsp Chili vinegar and 4 Tbsp soy sauce. Stir fry.&#10;6.  Add black pepper and stir fry for 30 seconds. \*\*Notes\*\*    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Bourbon Chicken\*\*By \[Preppy Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kMDxT\_fjJeI) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Lb Chicken Thighs or Breast, sliced into small pieces (1 Costco pack)&#10;  - 4-8 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1/3 Cup Bourbon&#10;  - 1/3 Cup Soy Sauce, regular or light&#10;  - 1/2 Cup Chicken stock&#10;  - 1/3 Cup Brown sugar&#10;  - 1/2 Tsp Onion powder&#10;  - 1/2 Tsp Ground or fresh Ginger&#10;  - 1/4 Tsp Red Pepper Flakes&#10;  - 3 Green onions stalks, thinly sliced     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Coat chicken pieces in cornstarch.&#10;2.  Mix other ingredients to make sauce.&#10;3.  Heat pan on high  olive oil in pan (\\\~2 Tbsp)&#10;4.  Add chicken and cook a couple minutes on each side until cooked through. Nice browning. Once the edges are nice and golden, flip over. Then transfer to a bowl.&#10;5.  Add garlic and cook for 30-60 seconds until fragrant.&#10;6.  Add sauce ingredients and stir onto pan. Reduce to low and once it stops bubbling. Add chicken and cook until sauce reduces and thickens up. Chicken should be coated glossy.&#10;7.  Add green onions at the end. \*\*Notes\*\*    |

| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Chicken Adobo\*\*Modified recipe by \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZdxSPFt4oxk\&t=350s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2-3 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2-3 Chicken thighs or breasts, sliced into cubes&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Neutral oil (Canola)&#10;  - 30-40 Peppercorns, whole&#10;  - 1 Pinch Ground black pepper&#10;  - 15-20 Garlic cloves, smashed&#10;  - 1 Cup White vinegar&#10;  - ⅔ Cup Filipino soy sauce&#10;      &#10;      - Alternatives: Japanese shoyu or mix of Chinese dark and light soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Cup brown sugar or palm sugar&#10;  - 2-3 Bay leaves     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Season chicken lightly with salt.&#10;2.  Heat oil in a wok or pot over medium-high heat until it shimmers. Add chicken and cook, turning occasionally until lightly brown.&#10;3.  Add ground pepper, peppercorns, and garlic. Stir to combine.&#10;4.  Add vinegar, soy sauce, sugar, and bay leaves. Stir to combine.&#10;5.  Bring to a boil, and then reduce to a bare simmer. Cover and cook for 3 minutes. &#10;6.  Stir and cook for another 1-2 minutes. &#10;7.  Check to ensure the temperature inside chicken is at least 155 degrees F. Cook until the chicken is fully tender.&#10;8.  Serve with white rice. \*\*Notes\*\*Can use full skin-on chicken thighs or drumsticks that are not sliced, but when boiling with cover, will need to cook for 20-30 minutes.   |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Chicken Cutlet\*\*By \[Aaron and Claire\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZfF7yTbc4fY\&t=214s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Chicken thighs or breasts&#10;  - Flour&#10;  - Eggs&#10;  - Panko bread crumbs     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Slice breast in half horizontally. Mash flat with mallet into ¼ inch thickness.&#10;2.  Season both sides with salt and pepper.&#10;3.  Take 3 dredging trays and fill each with flour, eggs, and bread crumbs.&#10;4.  Dip chicken into flour and shake off excess. Dip into egg then into bread crumbs. Cover with a good amount of crumbs and gently press down on chicken to ensure bread crumbs stick.&#10;5.  Fill the pan with enough oil to cover chicken. Heat oil to 320-340  °F. Fry for about 6-8 minutes.&#10;6.  Let rest on a cooling rack. \*\*Notes\*\*For chicken katsu, can serve with \[Katsu Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.9fq1v5z88ryg) and lemon.Can serve with onions, green onions, eggs, dashi. Katsu don?     |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Green Curry\*\* By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LIbKVpBQKJI\&t=193s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Chicken thighs or breast, sliced into cubes&#10;  - Any vegetable&#10;  - Red bell pepper&#10;  - Chilis&#10;  - 1 Can Coconut milk&#10;  - Chicken stock.&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Green curry paste&#10;  - 1-2 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 2-3 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Cup Thai basil&#10;  - 3-4 Lime leaves    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a pot, boil ½ cup coconut milk. Add Thai green curry paste. Could add less paste if too spicy.&#10;2.  Cook until reduced until you see coconut oil separated from the paste. Should be thick in consistency.&#10;3.  Add chicken thigh and mix. If using breasts, cook for less time.&#10;4.  Add 1 cup coconut milk, and 1 cup of chicken stock. Ratio of chicken stock is up to you, if you want lighter, more chicken stock. If more rich, more coconut milk.&#10;5.  Season with 1 Tbsp fish sauce and sugar. Add lime leaves. Simmer gently for about 15 minutes until the chicken is fork tender.&#10;6.  Add vegetables and red bell pepper, chilis before the chicken is done. Timing may vary depending on the vegetable. &#10;7.  Cook and gently simmer, not boiling.&#10;8.  Taste and adjust seasoning with fish sauce. Taste should be strong because it will be diluted with rice.&#10;9.  Finish with thai basil. \*\*Notes\*\*Could add \\\~10 mashed basil leaves to increase green color.  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Japanese Curry\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QhsrBz9Cceg) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Chicken thighs or breast, sliced into cubes&#10;  - 1 Potato, peeled and sliced into chunks&#10;  - 1 Carrot, peeled and sliced into chunks&#10;  - ½ Onion, sliced&#10;  - 4 Garlic cloves, minced &#10;  - 1 Ginger nub, minced&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Curry powder (S\\\&B brand)&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Flour&#10;  - 2-3 Cups Chicken broth&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Honey    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat the pan on medium-high  with 4 Tbsp of  oil or butter.&#10;2.  Add chicken and fry. Add salt and pepper. Cook for a few minutes.&#10;3.  Add onions and fry for a few minutes.&#10;4.  Add garlic and ginger. Fry for a minute until aromatic.&#10;5.  Add curry powder and flour. Toast for a minute.&#10;6.  Add chicken broth.&#10;7.  Add potatoes and carrots. Cook until simmer.&#10;8.  Add honey.&#10;9.  Can add water or more chicken broth if too thick. &#10;10. Raise to high heat and simmer, then lower and cook for about 10 minutes. Cook until potatoes are ready.&#10;11. Season with salt and pepper, and MSG.&#10;12. Serve with rice.&#10;13. Can add Fish sauce for seasoning.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Japanese Curry\*\*By \[Just One Cookbook\](https://www.justonecookbook.com/simple-chicken-curry/) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1-2 Chicken thighs or breast, sliced into cubes&#10;  - 1-3 Potato, peeled and sliced into chunks&#10;  - 2-3 Carrots, peeled and sliced into chunks&#10;  - ½ Apple, minced or grated&#10;  - 1-2 Onion, sliced into wedges&#10;  - 4 Garlic cloves, minced or grated&#10;  - 1 Ginger nub, minced or grated&#10;  - 2 Cubes curry roux (S\\\&B brand)&#10;  - 4 Cups Chicken broth&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Honey&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Ketchup    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  On a medium heat pot with oil, add onions. Cook for 5 minutes. Can cook for another 5 minutes if time permits so onions are caramelized.&#10;2.  Add garlic and ginger. Mix.&#10;3.  Add chicken and stir fry until no longer pink on the outside. Can lower heat if onions are getting too brown..&#10;4.  Add chicken broth, apple, honey, soy sauce, and ketchup.&#10;5.  Add carrots and potatoes (If russet potatoes, add in the last 15-20 minutes of cooking). Broth should barely cover the ingredients.&#10;6.  Simmer covered on medium-low heat for 15 minutes. Can simmer uncovered if ingredients are completely submerged in broth.&#10;7.  Skim scum and foam from the surface of broth.&#10;8.  Continue to cook until carrots and potatoes can be pierced with a fork easily.&#10;9.  Turn off the heat and add curry roux. Slowly let it dissolve in a spoon.&#10;10. Simmer uncovered on medium low while stirring frequently until thick, typically about 5-10 minutes. If too thick, add water.&#10;11. Season with salt and pepper, and MSG. Can add curry spice if needed. \*\*Notes\*\*Could be served with beni-shoga (pickled ginger). To reheat, add water.Can substitute meat with beef, pork, shrimp tofu, mushrooms. Can substitute apple with banana, orange/apricot jam, mirin, or apple sauce.For refreshing/sour taste, can add lemon, tomato, or yogurt.For spicy, can add red chili pepper, black pepper, cayenne pepper.For fragrance, can add cumin, cinnamon, bay leaf, curr leaves.For umami and depth, can add butter, chocolate, garlic cloves, instant coffee, milk, oyster sauce, red wine, sake, tonkatsu sauce, worcestershire sauce. |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Mushroom Chicken\*\*By \[Tess Cooks 4u\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RnWL901Y\_BI\&t=143s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Lb Chicken Thighs or Breast, sliced into small pieces (1 Costco pack)&#10;  - 1 Package Mushrooms, cut into quarters&#10;  - 1 Cup Zucchini&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Ginger, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp cornstarch, 2 Tbsp water slurry\*\*Marinade:\*\*  - 4 Tbsp Light Soy Sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Chinese cooking wine&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 3 Tbsp  Cornstarch\*\*Sauce:\*\*  - ½ Cup Chicken broth or water with ¼ Tsp Bouillon&#10;  - ¼ Cup Light soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Chinese cooking wine or Rice wine vinegar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Brown sugar | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To chicken, add  marinade ingredients. Mix and marinate for 30 minutes.&#10;2.  Boil mushrooms and zucchini, blanch for 30 seconds.&#10;3.  Pan on high heat with oil. Add chicken and stir fry for 5-7 minutes or until cooked. Remove chicken from pan.&#10;4.  Then cook garlic and ginger. Cook and stir for 20-30 seconds, then add sauce ingredients. Simmer for 1-2 minutes. &#10;5.  Then add blanched vegetables and chicken. Add some cornstarch slurry to thicken sauce, a little at a time. \*\*Notes\*\*    |

| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Oyakodon\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez Alt\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NcXzpCqKdUA) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Chicken Breasts or thighs, sliced into bite sized pieces&#10;  - 3 Eggs&#10;  - ½ Onion, sliced&#10;  - 3 Tsp Hondashi &#10;  - 4 Tbsp Dry sake &#10;  - 2 Tbsp Light Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Mirin (or 1 Tbsp Sugar)&#10;  - 1 Stalks Green onion, chopped&#10;  - Shichimi togarashi (Japanese chili powder)     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To pan, add dashi stock, sake, soy sauce, sugar, chicken, and onions. Bring to a simmer on medium heat and cover.&#10;2.  To bowl, add egg. Could add salt, or soy sauce + dashi powder + green onions. Beat it.&#10;3.  Flip chicken. Cover again.  Total cooking of chicken about 7 minutes.&#10;4.  Once chicken is cooked. Pour egg over the top. Cover and cook for 15-30 seconds.&#10;5.  Adjust with sugar or soy sauce per preference.&#10;6.  Serve over rice with eggs and shichimi togarashi. \*\*Notes\*\*Could be served with beni-shoga (pickled ginger).     |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Poached Chicken with Ginger Scallion Sauce\*\*By Junmac |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2-3 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2-3 Chicken thighs or breasts&#10;  - Salt & Pepper to taste&#10;  - \[Ginger Scallion Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.b9wyhcfdkukn)     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Start with cold water in a pot to cover ingredients.&#10;2.  Add chicken and salt/pepper  to the pot. (Optional: Add garlic, ginger, onions).&#10;3.  Cook on high heat until the pot starts to simmer, then low to medium heat immediately.&#10;4.  For a gentle poach, avoid boiling or simmering.&#10;5.  Cook for 10 minutes on medium heat. Check with a thermometer that chicken is cooked to 160 degrees F.&#10;6.  Serve with white rice and Ginger Scallion Sauce. \*\*Notes\*\*Can prepare Ginger Scallion Sauce while poaching chicken.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |

| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Chicken thighs or breast, sliced into cubes     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Coat chicken with paprika, cumin, dehydrated onion, dehydrated garlic, salt, and pepper. Cook. Shred.&#10;2.  Prepare \[avocado cilantro dressing\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.qgqf1z23wk71) with boiled or roasted onion and jalapeno blended in.&#10;3. Toast with tortillas lightly fried with oil per preference.&#10;5.  Serve with lime and cilantro.&#10;6.  Can serve with tortilla chips (deep fried corn tortillas at 375 degrees until no longer bubbling, salted) and pico de gallo.   |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Red Curry\*\* By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xsyrSvUCW\_A\&t=304s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Chicken thighs or breast, sliced into cubes&#10;  - Squash or any vegetable&#10;  - 1 Can Coconut milk&#10;  - Chicken stock.&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Red curry paste&#10;  - 1-2 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 2-3 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Cup thai basil    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In pot, boil ½ cup coconut milk. Add thai red curry paste. Could add less paste if too spicy.&#10;2.  Cook until reduced until you see coconut oil separated from the paste. Should be thick in consistency.&#10;3.  Add chicken thigh. If using breasts, cook for less time.&#10;4.  Add 1 cup coconut milk, and 1 cup of chicken stock. Ratio of chicken stock is up to you, if want lighter, more chicken stock. If more rich, more coconut milk.&#10;5.  Season with 1 Tbsp fish sauce and sugar. Simmer gently for about 15 minutes until chicken is fork tender.&#10;6.  About 8 minutes before chicken is done, add squash. May vary depending on vegetable. Can overcook quickly.&#10;7.  Cook and gently simmer, not boiling.&#10;8.  Taste and adjust seasoning. Taste should be strong because will be diluted with rice.&#10;9.  Finish with thai basil.&#10;10. Could also add bell peppers or something red.   |

Will see oil seep out of edges.&#10;4.  Add chicken and mix. Add rest of coconut milk.&#10;5.  Add vegetables and stir fry.&#10;6.  Add tomatoes or red bell pepper at the end.&#10;7.  Taste and adjust with fish sauce.&#10;8.  Finish with thai basil. \*\*Notes\*\*Could  |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Air Fried Chicken Wings\*\*By \[Domestic Superhero\](https://domesticsuperhero.com/air-fryer-chicken-wings-super-crispy/) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 lb Chicken wings (\\\~10 wings)&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Olive oil&#10;  - 1 Tsp Garlic powder&#10;  - 1 Tsp Onion powder&#10;  - 1 Tsp Paprika&#10;  - 1 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1 Tsp Black pepperOptional:  - \[Buffalo Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.rnl4c9ve3etb)&#10;  - \[Buttermilk Ranch\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.5mabfxdl4ena)     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Pat dry chicken wings with clean paper towels. &#10;2.  Once the wings are dry, add to the mixing bowl. Add all ingredients to the bowl.&#10;3.  Toss chicken until coated. &#10;4.  Preheat the air fryer to 400 G for 2 minutes. &#10;5.  If using buffalo sauce, place wings in a mixing bowl, cover with Buffalo Sauce, and toss together to coat.&#10;6.  Can serve with Buttermilk Ranch and/or celery. \*\*Notes\*\*Can coat with \[Honey BBQ Buffalo  Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.e6gsufbwbj9w) instead of Buffalo sauce.   |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Baked Buffalo Chicken Wings\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mh2AXh1eRmE\&t=1197s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2.5 lb Chicken wings (\\\~20 wings)&#10;  - 2.5 Tsp Kosher salt or 1.25 Tsp if using table salt&#10;  - 2.5 Tsp Baking powder&#10;  - 2.5 Tsp Cornstarch&#10;  - \[Buffalo Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.rnl4c9ve3etb)&#10;  - \[Buttermilk Ranch\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.5mabfxdl4ena)     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  If using water chilled chicken wings, will want to let it rest 8-24 hours.&#10;2.  If air chilled, can pat dry chicken wings with clean paper towels. &#10;3.  Once the wings are dry, add to the mixing bowl. Add salt, baking powder, and cornstarch to the bowl.&#10;4.  Toss chicken until coated. Coat should be dry and not pasty.&#10;5.  Put wings onto a cooking rack skin side up.&#10;6.  Place in the refrigerator for at least 8 hours.&#10;7.  Cook chicken in the oven at 450 degrees F for 15-30  minutes.&#10;8.  Prepare buffalo sauce.&#10;9.  Wings should be starting to get crispy and brown.&#10;10. Flip wings and put back into the oven for another 15-30 minutes. Wings should be crispy.&#10;11. Place wings in a mixing bowl, cover with Buffalo Sauce, and toss together to coat.&#10;12. Serve with Buttermilk Ranch and/or celery. \*\*Notes\*\*Can coat with \[Honey BBQ Buffalo  Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.e6gsufbwbj9w) instead of Buffalo sauce..   |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Baked Honey Sriracha Chicken Wings\*\*By \[Food Wishes\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BDVg0u2YQ7Q) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2.5 lb Chicken wings (\\\~20 wings)&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Kosher salt &#10;  - 1 Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - 1 Tsp Smoked paprika&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Baking powder (Aluminum free)&#10;  - \[Honey Sriracha Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.202jejr9yics)    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  If using water chilled chicken wings, will want to let it rest 8-24 hours.&#10;2.  If air chilled, can pat dry chicken wings with clean paper towels. &#10;3.  Once the wings are dry, add to the mixing bowl. Add salt, black pepper, paprika, and baking powder.&#10;4.  Put wings onto a cooking rack skin side up.&#10;5.  Cook chicken in the oven at 425 degrees F, turning every 15-20 minutes, until they are browned and crispy. Total cooking time will be about 1 hour, but it depends on the size and temperature of the wings..&#10;6.  Prepare Honey Sriracha sauce.&#10;7.  Place wings in a mixing bowl, cover with Honey Sriracha Sauce, and toss together to coat. \*\*Notes\*\*    |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Chicken Wing Tom Yum Soup\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LcxLAB18TNY) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2-3 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1.5 Lb Chicken wings&#10;  - 3-4 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 2-3 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Cup Shallots, coarsely chopped&#10;      &#10;      - Substitute: Onions&#10;  - 2 Stalks Lemon grass, smashed then cut into 2 inch piece, use base&#10;  - 10 Thin slices Galangal&#10;  - 10 Makrut lime leaves&#10;  - 5 Dried chilis, toasted&#10;      &#10;      - Or fresh chilis&#10;      - Or chili flakes, toasted&#10;  - 3.5 Oz Oyster mushrooms or Shimeji mushrooms&#10;  - 1.5 Cups Tomatoes, wedges&#10;  - 3-4 Tbsp Lime juice&#10;  - 7-8 Sprigs Cilantro&#10;      &#10;      - Substitute: Green onions  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Boil 5 cups of  water in a pot, then add chicken.&#10;2.  Add fish sauce, sugar, and shallots. Simmer for 20 minutes.&#10;3.  Add lemon grass and galangal. Crush Makrut lime leaves prior to putting in to soup.&#10;4.  Break open chili &#10;5.  Add mushrooms and simmer for 5 minutes.&#10;6.  Add tomatoes and simmer for 2 minutes.&#10;7.  Turn off, add lime juice and cilantro. \*\*Notes\*\*Galangal, lemon grass, and lime leaves are not edible.   |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Gaeng Jued (Bland soup)\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LcxLAB18TNY) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2-3 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1.5 Lb Chicken wings&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Pork spare ribs, cut into chunks&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - ½ Tsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp White pepper&#10;  - 5-6 Cilantro stems&#10;  - 1 2-inch section Daikon or ¼ Onion, diced&#10;  - 1.4 Oz Glass noodles  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Boil 5 cups of  water in a pot, then add chicken.&#10;2.  Add soy sauce, fish sauce, sugar, and white pepper.&#10;3.  Simmer for 5-10 minutes and remove scum.&#10;4.  Add cilantro stems and daikon.&#10;5.  Loosely cover the pot and let simmer until the meat is fork tender for a total of 20 minutes. Pork spare ribs should simmer for 30-40 minutes.&#10;6.  Add mushrooms and simmer for 5 minutes.&#10;7.  Add tomatoes and simmer for 2 minutes.&#10;8.  Turn off, add lime juice and cilantro.&#10;9.  Soak glass noodles in water for 7-10 minutes until soft and pliable. Cut in half. &#10;10. To make ½ lb meat balls, ground pork and ground chicken, 2 Tsp Soy sauce, 1 Tsp fish sauce, ¾ Tsp sugar, few cracks  white pepper. Could add garlic. Mix. Optional.&#10;11. Remove cilantro stems.&#10;12. Bring up to boil, add meatballs in.&#10;13. Add napa cabbage.&#10;14. Add glass noodles, only takes 3 minutes to cook.&#10;15. Top with fried garlic, garlic oil, and cilantro. \*\*Notes\*\*Very flexible with vegetables and protein: napa cabbage, carrots, cucumber, mushrooms, baby corn.Protein is flexible with: pork meatballs, asian meatballs, tofu, thai omelet.Glass noodles can last longer in refrigerator than other noodles.   |

| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Soy Sauce Chicken Wings\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBWm3ymf72c) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2-3 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1.5 Lb Chicken wings&#10;  - 1 Nub Ginger, thinly sliced&#10;  - 3 Cloves Garlic, thinly sliced\*\*For Sauce:\*\*  - 2 Tbsp Oyster Sauce&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Light Soy Sauce&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Dark Soy Sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Shaoxing Rice Wine&#10;  - ¼ Tbsp White Pepper&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Brown or White Sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  - ½ Cup Water  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Prepare sauce by mixing all ingredients listed.&#10;2.  Cut into thickest parts of chicken to help cook faster.&#10;3.  Next, blanch the wings, first boil 6 cups of hot water in a pot. Add wings to the pot and cook for 1.5 minutes.&#10;4.  Remove wings and set aside.&#10;5.  Remove water from the pot and dry.&#10;6.  Add 1 Tbsp oil and add ginger and garlic. Stir fry for 1 minute.&#10;7.  Add ½ Cup water and cover to simmer on low for 2 minutes.&#10;8.  Add half of the sauce mixture, and add in wings. Then add sauce over the wings.&#10;9.  Lower heat so that it is not boiling too quickly. Cover and simmer for 10 minutes.&#10;10. Uncover, flip wings. Cover and simmer again for 2 minutes. Uncover, and baste wings with sauce on high heat for 6 minutes.&#10;11. Plate drum sticks and serve with sauce in a small bowl. \*\*Notes\*\*    |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Tomato Chicken (Indian)\*\*By \[TasteRecipeHut\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2a6AsIOff5E) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2-3 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 7.5 g Chicken (\\\~15 wings)&#10;  - 1-2 Onions, sliced&#10;  - 3 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Nub Ginger, minced&#10;  - 1-2 Tomatoes, chopped&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Cilantro, chopped&#10;  - 1 Bay leaf&#10;  - 1 Inch Cinnamon stick&#10;  - 3 Pods Cardamom&#10;  - 4 Pods Clove&#10;  - ⅓ Tsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Cup Tomato paste&#10;  - ⅓ Tsp Turmeric powder&#10;  - 1 Tsp Coriander powder&#10;  - ⅔ Tsp Cumin powder&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Black pepper powder&#10;  - Salt to taste&#10;  - 1 Tsp Red chili powder&#10;  - ½ Tsp Garam masala&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tbsp Lemon juice&#10;  - Optional: 1 Cup Chicken stock | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Marinate chicken  for 20 minutes.\*\*For Marinade:\*\*1\.  ½ Tsp Turmeric powder&#10;2.  ½ Tsp Black pepper&#10;3.  Salt to taste&#10;4.  Fry chicken in a pot with oil on medium high heat until golden brown. Set aside chicken.&#10;5.  To pot, add 2 Tbsp oil, bay leaf, cinnamon stick, cardamom pods, clove pods, and sugar. Cook until sugar is caramelized.&#10;6.  Add onion and cook until golden.&#10;7.  Add ginger and garlic. Cook for 1 minute.&#10;8.  Add tomato paste. Cook for 1 minute.&#10;9.  Add turmeric powder, coriander powder, and cumin powder. Cook for 1 minute.&#10;10. Add black pepper, salt, red chili powder, and garam masala. Cook until oil separates.&#10;11. Add in fried chicken and stir.&#10;12. Add 1 cup of water or chicken stock,  and stir. Cover and cook on medium to low heat for 15 minutes.&#10;13. Add in tomato and stir.&#10;14. Add in cilantro and stir. \*\*Notes\*\*Can finish with lemon juice and/or MSG  to taste.Can substitute with rotisserie chicken, but cook chicken for a shorter period. Use chicken stock instead of water.    |

| :-: | :-: |

| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1.5 lb Chicken Wings&#10;  - 1 Sayote, chopped&#10;  - 1 Onion, chopped&#10;  - 1 Nub Ginger, sliced or minced&#10;  - 2 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - Spinach&#10;  - 1-2 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - ½ Cube of Knorr Chicken powder or ½ Tsp Chicken bouillon         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\. Turn on high heat until boiling. Then lower to a simmer and cook for 20 minutes.&#10;6.  Add sayote and cook for 10 minutes.&#10;7.  Add spinach and cook for 2 minutes.&#10;8.  Add fish sauce. \*\*Notes\*\*Can season with fish oil, soy sauce, sriracha, and sesame oil per preference.     |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Stir Fried Tomatoes and Eggs\*\*By \[Aaron and Claire\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uKXH489i-ok\&t=199s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 4 Eggs, large&#10;  - 2 Tomatoes, sliced into wedges or small pieces&#10;  - Salt&#10;  - Sugar&#10;  - MSG&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Oyster Sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Green onion stalks, thinly sliced     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Crack eggs into a mixing bowl. Add a pinch of salt, sugar, and MSG. Beat the eggs.&#10;2.  In a separate bowl, mix oyster sauce and soy sauce.&#10;3.  Add oil to a pan/wok on high heat.&#10;4.  Once hot, add eggs to the pan and gently fold the eggs. When soft and a little runny, remove eggs and set aside.&#10;5.  Add oil and green onions into the pan. Add tomatoes and a pinch of salt. Pour the oyster sauce and soy sauce around the pan and stir-fry tomatoes gently.&#10;6.  Once it starts to bubble, add in the eggs and gently break it apart. Stir-fry for two minutes.&#10;7.  Serve over rice and add green onions on top. \*\*Notes\*\*Can cook with additional ingredients, including mushrooms, onions, garlic, and cilantro.   |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Spanish Garlic Eggs\*\*By \[Spain on a Fork\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SE6WkS8ivSE) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Eggs, large&#10;  - ¼ Cup Extra virgin olive oil&#10;  - 2-4 Garlic cloves, thinly sliced \*\*For Garlic Parsley Oil:\*\*  - 2 Cloves Garlic cloves, thinly sliced&#10;  - 1 Pinch Salt&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Parsley, minced&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ⅓ Tbsp Dried Parsley&#10;      - 2 Tbsp Extra virgin olive oil     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To make the garlic parsley oil, to a mortar add garlic, salt, and parsley. Mash until it has a paste-like texture. Add extra virgin olive oil and mix. Set aside for now..&#10;2.  Heat the pan on medium heat with extra virgin olive oil.&#10;3.  Add garlic to the pan and saute for 30 seconds. Garlic should be lightly brown.&#10;4.  Crack eggs into a pan. Incorporate the slices of garlic into eggs and fry for 1-2 minutes until a golden crust forms on the bottom of the eggs. While cooking, splash oil on top of eggs to cook.&#10;5.  Remove eggs from the frying pan and remove excess oil. Plate.&#10;6.  Season eggs with salt and pepper. Top with garlic parsley oil. \*\*Notes\*\*As an alternative to the mortar, could use a handheld blender or food processor to prepare the olive oil topping. |

Seasoned soy sauce&#10;      &#10;      - Heat pan with neutral oil&#10;      - Add shallots until wilted&#10;      - Add scallions and cilantro&#10;      - Light soy sauce&#10;      - Add water to help dilute&#10;      - Add dark soil sauce for color&#10;      - Maggi seasoning (MSG?), chicken bouillon&#10;      - White sugar&#10;      - Pinch of white pepper&#10;      - Simmer for 5 minutes and strain.&#10;2.  1/2 Cup cooked water, 2 eggs, pinch of salt&#10;3.  Add ½ Cup  dashi or chicken stock&#10;4.  Mix and add pinch of salt.&#10;5.  Add over sieve to microwavable bowl.&#10;6.  Stir. Use spoon to remove bubbles.&#10;7.  Cover with plastic wrap. Poke a few holes.&#10;8.  Cook for 2 minutes and add 30 second increments until cooked to preference.&#10;9.  Top with soy sauce and green onions   |

With some oil in pan, add ginger and cook for 10 seconds. Add green onions, garlic, and chilis. Stir fry until aromatic.&#10;5.  Add sauce. Once boiling, add eggs to pan at a low simmer.&#10;6.  Toss in sauce at a low simmer for a 2 minutes.&#10;7.  Serve with rice. \*\*Notes\*\*1   |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Braised Pork (Luo Fan)\*\*By \[Spice N’ Pans\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hqcVtqGoRG0\&t=306s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 500 g Pork belly, thinly sliced&#10;  - 2 Shallots, minced&#10;  - 2 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 2 Mushrooms, minced&#10;  - 1 Tsp Five Spice powder&#10;  - Few dashes of white pepper&#10;  - ½ Cup Chinese white rice wine&#10;  - ⅓ Cup Light soy sauce&#10;  - ⅓ Cup Dark soy sauce&#10;  - 15 Grams Rock sugar (1 Tbsp)&#10;  - 700 ML chicken stock or chicken bouillon&#10;  - Hard boiled eggs        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Prepare hard boiled eggs.&#10;2.  Add a small amount of oil to the pan and heat on high. &#10;3.  Add pork and stir fry until lightly brown. Water will seep out of the pork, keep cooking until dry. Then cook until some oil comes out.&#10;4.  Add shallots and stir fry.&#10;5.  Add garlic and stir fry for about 30 seconds.&#10;6.  Add mushrooms, five spice, white pepper, and chinese white rice wine. Stir fry.&#10;7.  Add soy sauces. Add white sugar. Stir fry.&#10;8.  Add chicken stock. And cover until boiling.&#10;9.  Add hard boiled eggs.&#10;10. Cover and lower to low heat. Cook for 45-60 min.&#10;11. Could cook longer for more meat.&#10;12. Serve with rice and vegetables.      |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Chashu (For Ramen)\*\*By \[Kimono Mom\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iR8fXITKqAE) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 500 g Pork belly&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Sake&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Worcestershire sauce&#10;  - 200 mL (0.85 Cups)  water        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cook pork belly in a pan with oil to sear all sides.&#10;2.  Remove pork, then pour boiling water over pork to remove excess fat.&#10;3.  Wipe the oil from the pan and add sugar, soy sauce, sake, worcestershire sauce, and water.&#10;4.  Add pork and cook for 5 minutes with cover on. Flip and cook for another 5 minutes. &#10;5.  Slice \*\*Notes\*\*Can be served with \[ramen\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.gej0y56kg4zx).   |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Crispy Pork Belly\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A33iR\_Lp\_WQ\&t=311s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 500 g Pork belly,&#10;  - ½ Tsp Five spice powder&#10;  - ⅓ Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - ¼ Tsp White pepper&#10;  - 1 Tsp Garlic salt&#10;  - 2 Tsp Ground bean sauce (Optional)&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sugar (Optional)&#10;  - White vinegar or sweet rice vinegar        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To pot, boil 2 cups of water on high heat. Add pork belly skin down. After 1 minute flip and cook for 30 seconds. Remove and place in cool water. &#10;2.  Use a knife to scrape off skin to clean it. Dry with a paper towel.&#10;3.  Poke skin all over with a fork or tooth pick. This step is necessary for bubbling of skin. Just poke through the skin, not too deep.&#10;4.  Flip to meat side and score meat with a knife into 2 inch rectanlges.&#10;5.  Add five spice, black pepper, white pepper, and garlic salt to a bowl and mix. Rub seasoning mix onto meat side of pork belly, getting into gaps as well.&#10;6.  Optional: Add ground bean sauce and sugar to a bowl, mix, then paint onto meat side of pork belly.&#10;7.  Place meat onto foil, skin side up.&#10;8.  Sprinkle 1 Tbsp salt onto skin and rub it in. After 5 minutes, wipe off salt with a paper towel. Use a knife to scrape off all of salt.&#10;9.  Add vinegar to skin and spread onto it.&#10;10. Refrigerate for at least 10-12 hours.&#10;11. Heat oven to 375 degrees F. Place pork and foil on top of a cooling rack in a baking sheet. Add some water to the baking sheet. Place into oven and cook for 20-25 minutes.&#10;12. Turn up heat to 425 degrees F and cook for 30-35 minutes.&#10;13. Check on pork to make sure it is crispy and not burnt.&#10;14. Can check temp of pork to be a minimum of 145 degrees F.&#10;15. Remove pork and let rest for an hour before cutting along scores. \*\*Notes\*\*Can refrigerate for 2-3 days. Can freeze. To make crunchy, cook in oven or toaster again.Sauce: 1 Tbsp fish sauce, 1 Tbsp lime, Chili flakes, ¾ tsp Sugar, toasted rice powder, green onions or cilantroSauce: Toyomansi: 1-3 cloves garlic, 1-2 red chili, ¼ Cup calamansi juice, ¼ Cup soy sauce |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Al Pastor Tacos\*\*By \[Views on the Road\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mHbghPEgp14) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 lb Pork shoulder, thinly sliced&#10;  - 4-6 Chiles Guajillo, California or New Mexico Chile&#10;  - 1-2 Chile Pasillas&#10;  - 1 Box Achiote (3.5 oz)&#10;  - 1 Bay leaf&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Mexican oregano (Oregano entero)&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Cumin&#10;  - 1 ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  - 3-4 Garlic cloves&#10;  - ½ Tsp Ginger powder&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Cinnamon, ground&#10;  - 1 Tsp Clove powder or 2 Cloves&#10;  - 10 Peppercorns or ½ Tsp black pepper, ground&#10;  - ¾ Cup Apple cider vinegar or White vinegar&#10;  - 1 Cup Orange juice&#10;  - ¾ Cup Pineapple juice&#10;  - Optional: Juice of chipotle or chipotle pot | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cut stems of chiles and boil in hot water. Can remove seeds if preferred.&#10;2.  Place all ingredients, except the pork, into a blender to blend. &#10;3.  Marinate pork in marinade for up to 1-2 days.&#10;4.  Cook on a pan on high heat for 5 minutes, then flip and cook for another 5 minutes.&#10;5.  Serve as tacos with tortillas, salsa, chopped onions, limes, and cilantro. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with \[Spanish Rice\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.d4e46n1bvx5) and \[Refried Beans\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.k6ecoshkk3tb). |

| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Carnitas and Salsa\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez-Alt\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JRlmQdRN0G8) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 lb Pork shoulder, sliced into 2 inch cubes&#10;  - ½-1 Onion, sliced into quarters&#10;  - 1 Orange, slices into quarters&#10;  - 1 Cinnamon stick, broken into 3-4 pieces&#10;  - 5-6 Garlic cloves, smashed&#10;  - Oil, Canola\*\*For Salsa:\*\*  - 12 Tomatillos or 6 Roma tomatoes&#10;  - 1x Jalapeno pepper, sliced in half&#10;      &#10;      - Alternatives: Serrano or anaheim pepper&#10;  - 3-5 Garlic cloves&#10;  - Cilantro&#10;  - Big pinch Salt&#10;  - Lime   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Season pork with salt and place into a dutch oven pot so it fits in a single layer.&#10;2.  Add bay leaves and onion to the pot.&#10;3.  Squeeze orange juice over pork and then add the orange to the pot.&#10;4.  Add cinnamon and garlic.&#10;5.  Add enough oil so it is covering the top of the layer and covering all the cracks.&#10;6.  Cover pot and place into oven at 275 degrees F for 3 hours.&#10;7.  Once pork is tender enough, it should shred when pushed with a fork. At this point, can drain and eat or crisp pork up as follows.&#10;8.  Take pork pieces and place onto a metal sheet, then set aside for now.&#10;9.  Peel tomatillos (Do not peel if using roma tomatoes) and slice in half. Place them face down onto another metal sheet.&#10;10. Cut jalapeno in half and place face down onto the  metal sheet. May remove seeds and stem for less spicy. Can add more peppers for more spice. &#10;11. Smash 3-5 garlic cloves and add to the sheet.&#10;12. Set the oven to broil and place the metal sheet with the tomatillos, jalapeno, and garlic into the oven until charred. Then transfer to a blender.&#10;13. In the blender, also add onions from the dutch oven, a handful of cilantro (8-10 stems), a big pinch of salt, and juice from 1 lime. Blend roughly and ready serve. May adjust salt and lime as needed.&#10;14. Shred pork with a couple of forks and spread evenly onto metal sheet. Broil until crispy and brown on top.&#10;15. Can dip tortillas in carnitas braising liquid and heat on a skillet until pliable.&#10;16. Serve as tacos with tortillas, chopped onions, limes, and cilantro. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with \[Spanish Rice\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.d4e46n1bvx5) and \[Refried Beans\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.k6ecoshkk3tb). |

| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Char Siu\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=zkCoAKTbHpQ\&t=911s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 lb Pork belly or shoulder, long 1 inch slices, poked with a fork&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Honey\*\*For marinade:\*\*  - 1 Tsp Five spice powder&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Garlic salt&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Brown sugar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Light soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Red wine or beef stock&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Shaoxing wine (or Dry sherry, Mirin, sake)&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Hoisin sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - Optional: 1 Cube fermented bean curd      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Make marinade by mixing all marinade ingredients.&#10;2.  Marinade pork in marinade for 8-24 hours, not longer than 24 hours or the meat will get hard. Before cooking, let meat warm to room temp for at least 1 hour.&#10;3.  Place pork onto a baking rack on top over a baking tray lined with foil. Add 1-2 Tbsp of water to the tray.&#10;4.  Preheat oven to 425 degrees F &#10;5.  Cook pork  for 15 minutes and lather both sides with pork marinade.&#10;6.  Cook pork for 15 minutes and lather both sides with pork marinade.&#10;7.  Dilute honey with 2 Tsp water.&#10;8.  Cook pork for 10 minutes and lather both sides with diluted honey&#10;9.  Cook for 5 minutes at 450 degrees F. &#10;10. Lather both sides with diluted honey and let it cool for a few minutes before cutting into it.     |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Pork Tocino\*\*By \[Gideon General\](https://www.tiktok.com/@gidsgids/video/7120985503251434794?is\_from\_webapp=v1\&item\_id=7120985503251434794\&lang=en) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 lb Pork belly or shoulder, thinly sliced\*\*For marinade:\*\*  - ½-¾ Cup Brown sugar&#10;  - 8-10 Garlic cloves, diced or pressed.&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Filipino soy sauce&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Filipino white vinegar&#10;  - 1 Cup Pineapple juice&#10;  - 3 Tbsp kosher salt or 1.5 Tbsp of iodized/table salt&#10;  - 2 Tbsp ground black pepper&#10;  - Optional: 15-20 drops red food color or ¼ Cup Annatto water (Add hot water to annatto seeds to extract it’s color, then get ¼ cup of it)      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\. Serve with rice. \*\*Notes\*\*\*\*For Tocilog\*\* (Tocino breakfast meal), serve with:  - \[Garlic Fried Rice\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.rex0atktdww1)&#10;  - \[Sukang Sawsawan (Spiced Vinegar)\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.7q4tg0hf75ba)&#10;  - Fried egg&#10;  - Tomatoes (Roma preferred), chopped into small pieces      |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Caramelized Ground Pork\*\*By \[Recipe Tin Eats\](https://www.recipetineats.com/vietnamese-caramelised-pork-bowls/) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Lb Ground Pork (500 g)&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Chicken, Turkey, or Beef&#10;  - ½ Onion, diced&#10;  - 2 Tsp Ginger, minced&#10;  - 2 Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Thai or Birds eye chili, deseeded and minced&#10;  - Optional: 1 Stalk Lemongrass, white part only finely chopped  or Lemongrass paste&#10;  - 5 Tbsp Brown sugar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 1 Stalk Green onion, diced&#10;  - Cilantro, minced    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pan on high with oil.&#10;2.  Add onion, ginger, garlic, and chili (Lemongrass) and cook for 2 minutes. &#10;3.  Add porn and cook for 2 minutes or until white all over. Break up the meat with a wooden spoon.&#10;4.  Add sugar and fish sauce (Lemongrass paste). Stir and leave to cook until all juices cook out and pork starts to caramelize, about 2 minutes. &#10;5.  Stir and leave again for around 30 seconds to get more caramelization. Repeat two more times until caramelized to taste.&#10;6.  Top with green onions and cilantro&#10;7.  Serve with white rice. |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Garlic Longganisa\*\*By \[RV Manabat\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jNP5AwCUcns\&t=195s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Lb Ground pork&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Ground beef or chicken&#10;  - ½ Cup Dark brown sugar&#10;  - ½ Cup Garlic, minced&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Ground black pepper&#10;  - 2 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Flour&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Ketchup (Banana or Tomato)&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Annatto powder (Atsuete)      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add all ingredients to a mixing bowl and mix.&#10;2.  Take a scoop of meat and use parchment paper to shape meat into a sausage. Continue with the rest of the meat.&#10;3.  Let rest overnight.&#10;4.  Heat oil on low. Add Longganisa without overcrowding. Flipping occasionally.&#10;5.  Press on the sausage, and if it is firm, it is cooked already. Then cover the pan with lid to fully cook through. \*\*Notes\*\*\*\*For Longsilog\*\* (Longganisa breakfast meal), serve with:  - \[Garlic Fried Rice\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.rex0atktdww1)&#10;  - \[Sukang Sawsawan (Spiced Vinegar)\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.7q4tg0hf75ba)&#10;  - Fried egg&#10;  - Tomatoes (Roma preferred), chopped into small pieces      |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Mandu (Korean Dumplings)\*\*By Maanchi |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*\\\~80 Dumplings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Lb Ground pork&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Ground beef or chicken or mushrooms&#10;  - 4 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Tsp Ginger, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Tsp Ground Black Pepper&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sesame Oil&#10;  - 1lb Zucchini, thinly sliced into matchsticks&#10;  - 4 oz Garlic Chives, minced&#10;  - 1 Lb Tofu&#10;  - 1 Onion, minced&#10;  - Starch noodles or panko&#10;  - 1 Tsp salt&#10;  - Optional: 14 oz Hmong bean sprouts&#10;  - Dumpling Wrappers     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add ground pork, garlic, ginger, soy sauce, and black pepper to the pan and fry until cooked. Then add sesame oil. Set aside meat.&#10;2.  Cook noodles in boiling water. Cool with water and chop.&#10;3.  Add zucchini and onions to a bowl with the salt. Allow to sit. Squeeze out excess water then stir fry in a pan with 1 Tsp oil for 1 minute.&#10;4.  Blanch hmong bean sprouts for 1 minute. Cool with water and squeeze out water.&#10;5.  Squeeze out water from tofu.&#10;6.  Add 1 Tsp salt, pepper, 2 tsp sesame oil. Mix all ingredients.&#10;7.  Put water on outer edges of dumpling wrapper, add meat, then fold into the dumpling shape.Can fry, steam, or cook in soup (hondashi or veggie stock, salt, pepper, sesame oil, egg, garlic, green onions).Cook with oil that is hot (1-2 Tbsp). Add water and cover for 4-5 min. Then remove, add more oil to fry bottom. Flip to cook other side.\*\*Notes\*\*Can use cheesecloth to remove excess water.Thaw out dumpling wrappers night before.1 Tbsp soy sauce1 Tbsp VinegarSesame seedsHot pepper flakesGreen onions     |

| \#\#\# \*\*Salt and Pepper Pork Chops\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IjO33JveQro) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 lb Pork chops (Loin, Sirloin), sliced into ¼ inch thick pieces&#10;  - 1 Green onion, thinly sliced&#10;  - 2 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Cornstarch&#10;  - Optional: 1 Bell pepper, sliced into strips&#10;  - Optional: 1 Red chili pepper, thinly sliced\*\*For marinade:\*\*  - 2 Tbsp Cornstarch&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Cooking wine&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Light soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Water&#10;  - Mix ingredients, then add 1 Tsp Sugar and ½ Tsp Salt\*\*For seasoning:\*\*  - 1 Tbsp Garlic salt&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Salt &#10;  - ¼ Tsp White pepper  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Pound pork with the back of a knife to tenderize.&#10;2.  Add pork to marinade and mix for about 1 minute. Marinade for about 30-60 minutes.&#10;3.  Add 2 cups of oil to a pot. And set heat to obtain 360-400 °F&#10;4.  Add half a beaten egg to marinated pork and mix.&#10;5.  Add 3 Tbsp of cornstarch to a dredging dish.&#10;6.  Coat each piece of pork in cornstarch before adding it to the oil..&#10;7.  For the first fry, cook the pork chops in batches.  Fry pork chops at 360-400 °F for 1½ -2 minutes. Should be fried until light golden brown in color.&#10;8.  For the second fry, cook all pork chops at 430-470 °F for 30-40 seconds. Pork chops should be crisp..&#10;9.  Heat pan on medium heat.&#10;10. In the pan, add garlic, bell pepper, and red chili pepper for 30 seconds. Add pork and mix for 30 seconds. Add seasoning in batches while mixing. Cook for 15-20 seconds. Turn off heat. Mix until seasoning is evenly distributed for another 20 seconds. &#10;11. Serve with rice. \*\*Notes\*\*  |
