Do not place clams in water and do not put directly on ice. \*\*Notes\*\*\[Video on How to Clean Razor Clams\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GpDZhdU\_g5o)    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Cleaning Razor Clams and Frying\*\*By \[TheWDFW\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sTaRaHkFFEw) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  -    | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Rinse clams outside to remove sand.&#10;2.  Boil a pot of hot water. Dump clams into hot water for 10 seconds.&#10;3.  Dump clams out and rinse with cold water. &#10;4.  Remove shells.&#10;5.  To clean, cut off tip of neck.&#10;6.  Cut along the zipper through the siphon.&#10;7.  Cut through second siphon.&#10;8.  Cut off digger through gills. Separate gut material from digger. Cut through digger to remove intestinal material. &#10;9.  Rinse clam and digger under water to clean.&#10;10. Can store in the freezer in a bag with water for up to 6 months. \*\*Notes\*\*Lightly coat clam in flour and shallow fry in hot oil until a little brown. Don’t overcook otherwise it will be tough. Cook both sides. Season with salt and pepper. Can serve with lemon.   |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Clam Chowder\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P90jTVaA6YU) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Cans Clams or 7-8 Razor clams, cut into small dices&#10;  - 1 Russet Potato or Yukon gold potato, peeled and cut into small cubes&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - 1 Onion or Leek, minced&#10;  - 1-2 Stalks Celery, minced&#10;  - ¼ Cup Flour&#10;  - 4 Cups Whole Milk&#10;  - 1 Cup Clam juice&#10;  - 2 Bay leaves&#10;  - Black pepper&#10;  - Hot sauce (Tabasco)&#10;  - Westminster oyster crackers&#10;  - Optional: 4 Slices Bacon, cut into small dice | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  If using bacon, cook on medium heat until water evaporates, pork begins to brown and crisp in spots (7-10 minutes). Remove half and place onto paper towel. Then move on to step 2.&#10;2.  Heat butter in a pot on moderate heat. Add onions. Mix for 3 minutes.&#10;3.  Add celery and 1 tsp salt. Cook for about 1 minute or until onions and celery are soft.&#10;4.  Add flour to the pot and mix.&#10;5.  Add clam juice and stir.&#10;6.  Add milk and bay leaves. Bring to a simmer. If using canned clams, could add here.&#10;7.  Heat potatoes in water in a separate pot up to a simmer. Add salt. Cook for about 8 minutes. Potatoes should be al dente.&#10;8.  Add potatoes to the pot and cook for another 5 minutes or until potatoes are the preferred texture.&#10;9.  Turn off heat and add razor clam meat. Cover the pot for 1 minute to gently poach.&#10;10. Can take ¼ of soup and blend, or use a hand blender in soup until preferred consistency.&#10;11. Top with quite  a bit of black pepper.&#10;12. Adjust with salt or fish sauce per preference.&#10;13. Serve with oyster crackers, bacon, and Tabasco. \*\*Notes\*\*For richer chowder, could use ½ Cup Heavy cream in place of ½ Cup Milk.To make a corn chowder, could replace clams with corn and using chicken stock instead of clam juice.     |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Broiled Razor Clams\*\*By \[Rich D\](https://www.kxro.com/rich-ds-baked-clam-recipe/) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Clams&#10;  - Ritz Crackers&#10;  - Egg&#10;  - Butter, melted | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  1\\. Dip cleaned clams in the egg wash.&#10;2.  2\\. Coat well with crushed Ritz crackers.&#10;3.  3\\. Lay the clams out, single layer, on a greased baking sheet. (Lining the sheet with foil first makes easier clean up)&#10;4.  4\\. Drizzle the clams with melted butter, more or less to taste. (Without it you’ll get dry clams)&#10;5.    &#10;6.  Cooking:&#10;7.  1\\. Put the pan under the HOT broiler.&#10;8.  2\\. Broil 3 minutes. Turn over. Broil 3 more minutes, and they’re done. Avoid over-cooking or the clams get rubbery. \*\*Notes\*\*      |

  

|  |
| :-: |
| \# \*\*Salmon\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Fish and Chips\*\*By \[Babish Culinary University\](https://youtu.be/KObL442PWhQ?si=gFWPOShneI\_\_IjTT\&t=149) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - White fish (Cod or Haddock)      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.     \*\*Rinse and peel russet potatoes, then cut them into 10-15mm wide fries (or however you want to make them. Fries come in all different shapes and sizes).Place the fries into a pot with room temperature oil. Slowly bring the oil up to 350°F and keep stirring them until they are golden brown. Place them on paper towels to drain the excess oil.For the beer batter, combine 1 ½ cups of all purpose flour, ½ cup of corn starch, some paprika, a pinch of kosher salt, and a few twists of freshly ground pepper.Take 1 cup of that mixture to a tray, and set aside for dredging.In the main mixture add 1 tsp of baking powder and 1 Mexican beer and whisk to combine.Take your fish, dredge it in the powder mixture, and then add it to the beer batter.Slowly add to a pot with peanut or vegetable oil at 350°F, and cook for 6-9 minutes.Remove from oil, set on a paper towel to soak up the oil and to cool off. Serve with fries and enjoy\\\!\*\*    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Gochugaru Salmon\*\*By \[Erik Kim\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9ggrcoAvjns) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1-2 Salmon filets&#10;  - 1-2 Cups cooked rice&#10;  - 1 Cucumber, sliced\*\*For Sauce:\*\*  - 4 Tsp Gochugaru or Gochujang&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Maple Syrup or Honey&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Rice vinegar&#10;  - 1 Tsp Kosher salt      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Season salmon on all sides with salt and pepper.&#10;2.  Cook salmon on medium-high heat with the skin side down for 2-5 minutes. ⅔ of the salmon should be coral color.&#10;3.  Flip salmon and cook for 1-2 minutes. If needed, can cover and cook for a few minutes to fully cook through. Transfer salmon to a plate.&#10;4.  Add cooked rice to the fat in the pan and spread. Reduce heat to medium and cook until the bottom is lightly browned and toasted, about 5 minutes. Flip and cook for 1-2 min. Lightly salt. Transfer rice to another plate.&#10;5.  In the empty pan, pour sauce mixture in and cook, stirring constantly, over medium-high heat until it bubbles up and reduces significantly, 15 sec - 1 min.  It should look sticky, turn off heat and add cold butter, stirring until fully melted and incorporated. &#10;6.  Pour glaze over salmon and serve with cucumbers.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Honey Garlic Salmon\*\*By \[Jerene’s Eats\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vAziD9FWF08) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1-2 Salmon filets&#10;  - Salt & Pepper to taste&#10;  - 1 Tsp Paprika&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Olive oil&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Lemon juice&#10;  - ½-¼ Cup Honey&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - 4 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - Green onion/chives/parsley, finely chopped&#10;  - Optional: Sliced lemon      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a bowl, mix honey, soy sauce, and lemon juice.&#10;2.  Season salmon with salt, pepper, and paprika.&#10;3.  Heat pan on medium heat with olive oil.&#10;4.  Cook salmon for 3-4 minutes until golden brown.&#10;5.  Flip salmon and cook for 3-4 minutes.&#10;6.  Add butter and garlic to the pan.&#10;7.  Cook until slightly golden.&#10;8.  Add the mixture of honey, soy sauce, and lemon juice to the pan.&#10;9.  Spoon sauce from pan onto top of salmon to baste as it cooks.&#10;10. Once finished, add green onions, chives, or parsley once to add color.&#10;11. Can add sliced lemon to garnish.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Mayonnaise Tamarind Baked Salmon\*\*By Mama Tess |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1-2 Salmon filets&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Mayonnaise&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Tamarind paste/concentrate&#10;  - Salt and pepper to taste      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Preheat oven to 325 °F.&#10;2.  Season salmon with salt and pepper.&#10;3.  Add Mayonnaise to the top of the salmon.&#10;4.  Top with Tamarind concentrate.&#10;5.  Cook salmon in the oven at 325 °F for 18-25 minutes. (125 °F internal temperature).    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Miso Glazed Salmon with Teriyaki Sauce\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez-Alt\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=51GpBG4iJcA) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1-2 Salmon filets&#10;  - ¼ Cup White miso paste&#10;      &#10;      - Alternatives: Red or yellow miso paste&#10;  - ⅓ Cup sake&#10;      &#10;      - Alternatives: broth/water with a dash of vinegar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ¼ Cup brown sugar or honey&#10;  - 2 Tbsp oil&#10;  - \[Teriyaki Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.myc3no5vq6uy)&#10;  - Sesame seeds (Black and white)      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add miso paste, sake, soy sauce, sugar, and oil to a mixing bowl and mix.&#10;2.  Mix salmon in marinade. Can cook immediately or marinade for up to 2 days.&#10;3.  Place salmon on a foil lined tray.&#10;4.  Broil on high heat for 10 minutes. May vary depending on oven and thickness of salmon.&#10;5.  Remove salmon from the oven and let sit for a few minutes.&#10;6.  Glaze with Teriyaki Sauce.&#10;7.  Top with sesame seeds and serve with white rice. \*\*Notes\*\*Longer the marinade process, the more moist and firm the flesh will be.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Salmon Crusted with Bread Crumbs\*\*By \[Babish Culinary University\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KObL442PWhQ) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1-2 Salmon filets&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - 2 Cups Panko bread crumbs&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Fresh Thyme, chopped&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Fresh Rosemary, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Grain mustard&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Mayonnaise&#10;  - 1 Egg      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a pan on medium-high heat, melt the butter.&#10;2.  Then add bread crumbs and toast until slightly golden brown. &#10;3.  Add thyme and rosemary, and cook for another 30 seconds. &#10;4.  Set aside bread crumbs, and season with salt and pepper.&#10;5.  Make a 1 part grain mustard and 1 part mayonnaise mix.&#10;6.  Place salmon filets onto foil coated with nonstick spray or vegetable oil. Season salmon with salt and pepper.&#10;7.  Coat top of salmon filets with the mustard/mayonnaise.&#10;8.  Add 1 egg white to the bread crumbs and mix.&#10;9.  Add bread crumbs to the top of the salmon.&#10;10. Cook salmon in the oven at 325 °F for 18-25 minutes. (125 °F internal temperature).&#10;11. Let salmon cool for 5 minutes, then serve.     |

| \# \*\*Shrimp\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Garlic Shrimp\*\* By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mDIHYywQ5Fw) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 lb Shrimp&#10;  - 8 Cloves Garlic, smashed&#10;  - ¾ Tsp White pepper&#10;  - 2 ½ Tsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1 ½ Tsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 1 ½ Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 ½ Tsp Shrimp paste in oil&#10;  - 8 Cilantro stems, chopped&#10;  - Fried Garlic&#10;  - Chopped Cilantro or Green onions | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Devein, peel, shrimp.&#10;2.  Seer shrimp on high heat with oil. Once browned, flip and brown on both sides. Repeat if multiple batches.&#10;3.  Lower heat. Add garlic to oil. Add seasoning.&#10;4.  Add shrimp and cook.&#10;5.  Add sauces. Increase heat.&#10;6.  Reduce sauce a little, stir fry.&#10;7.  Serve with rice \*\*Notes\*\*Instead of shrimp paste, could cook head on shrimp.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Ginger Shrimp & Glass Noodles\*\* By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L6AZX8cb3ko\&t=323s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Shrimp&#10;  - Glass noodles&#10;  - ½ Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - ½  Tsp White pepper&#10;  - 6 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 2 Sprigs Cilantro stems&#10;  - 3 Nubs Ginger, sliced&#10;  - 1.5 Tbsp Vegetable oil&#10;  - 1 Stalk Green onions, sliced\*\*For Sauce:\*\*  - ½ Cup Chicken stock, unsalted&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Dark Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 2 Tsp Sugar | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Soak glass noodles in room temperature water to soften them up. Can cut noodles into smaller pieces. Drain out water.&#10;2.  Transfer noodles to a wide bowl and pour sauce over it. Toss noodles in sauce to coat.&#10;3.  Prepare shrimp by using scissors to cut off the long antenna and sharp  beak. Cut through the back of the shrimp and remove the vein.&#10;4.  Add shrimp to the noodle bowl and toss. Let shrimp sit on the bottom of the bowl for a few minutes. Toss again and put the noodles on the bottom.&#10;5.  To a small pot, with lid, add black pepper, white pepper, garlic, cilantro stems, ginger, and vegetable. Mix together, then cook on high until sizzling.&#10;6.  Then add noodles and shrimp to the pot. Place shrimp on top of noodles. &#10;7.  Cover until sauce is boiling, then turn down to medium-low. Cover and cook for 3 minutes.&#10;8.  Stir. Squeeze shrimp heads to get shrimp fat into sauce. Cover and cook for 2-3 minutes.&#10;9.  Top with green onions.&#10;10. Serve with rice \*\*Notes\*\*Could use pork fat or pork belly instead of vegetable oil.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Panko Fried Shrimp\*\*By Taste of Asian Food |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Shrimp, peeled and deveined with tail still on, washed&#10;  - Flour&#10;  - Eggs&#10;  - Panko bread crumbs     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  (Optional step) Soak shrimp in water with 2 Tsp salt and 1 Tsp baking soda. Stir. Then rinse with water and pat dry.&#10;2.  Take 3 dredging trays and fill each with flour, eggs, and bread crumbs. To flour, can add salt, pepper, garlic powder, paprika, or turmeric.&#10;3.  Dip shrimp  into flour and shake off excess. Dip into egg then into bread crumbs. Cover with a good amount of crumbs and gently press down on shrimps to ensure bread crumbs stick.&#10;4.  Fill the pan with enough oil to cover shrimp. Heat oil to 320-340  °F. Fry for about 2-3 minutes. Shrimp should be golden brown. Do not overcrowd the pot.&#10;5.  Let rest on a cooling rack. \*\*Notes\*\*For chicken katsu, can serve with \[Katsu Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.9fq1v5z88ryg) and lemon.Can serve with hardboiled egg mixed with mayonnaise.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Tom Yum Goong (Creamy Shrimp)\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hhcYNjeQ\_XY) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2-3 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Shrimp&#10;  - ½ Cup (4 oz) Evaporated milk or coconut milk&#10;  - 3-4 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - ¼ Cup Thai chili paste&#10;  - 2 Stalks Lemongrass, smashed then cut into 2 inch piece, use base&#10;  - 10 Thin slices Galangal&#10;  - 10 Makrut lime leaves&#10;  - 5 Dried chilis, toasted&#10;      &#10;      - Or fresh chilis, mashed or finely minced&#10;      - Or chili flakes, toasted&#10;  - 3.5 Oz Oyster mushrooms or Shimeji/beech mushrooms&#10;  - ¼ Cup Lime juice&#10;  - 7-8 Sprigs Cilantro&#10;      &#10;      - Substitute: Green onions  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Prepare shrimp by removing the head. To devein, use a toothpick and poke under the second joint from the tail, then pull out the vein. Peel shrimp. Place heads and shell into pot.&#10;2.  Saute until all residual water evaporates and shrimp tamale is browning. Then deglaze with 4 cups of water and scrub the bottom of the pot. Allow to simmer for 15-20 minutes.&#10;3.  Fish out shrimp and add lemongrass and galangal. Crush/rip Makrut lime leaves prior to putting in to soup. Add chili. Cook for about 5 minutes. Can remove some or all herbs.&#10;4.  Add evaporated milk.&#10;5.  Add mushrooms. Cook for 1 minute.&#10;6.  Add fish sauce and Thai chili paste.&#10;7.  Add shrimp. Cook for 30 seconds and turn off heat.&#10;8.  Allow to cool for a minute so not building or simmering, then add lime while stirring to avoid curdling.&#10;9.  Add cilantro.&#10;10. Turn off, add lime juice and cilantro. \*\*Notes\*\*Galangal, lemon grass, and lime leaves are not edible.Chili paste in oil (Mae Ploy)   |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Spam Musubi Stir Fry\*\*By Junmac |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Can Spam, cut into bite sized pieces&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Mirin&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - Furikake&#10;  - Nori | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Fry spam in a hot pan until preferred browning. Set aside.&#10;2.  Add mirin and stir to get flavoring that is on the pan.&#10;3.  Add soy sauce and sugar. Stir until sugar is melted.&#10;4.  Add spam back to the pan to coat in sauce.&#10;5.  Set aside and top with furikake and nori.&#10;6.  Serve over rice. \*\*Notes\*\*      |

|  |

  

|  |  |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Braised Tofu (Dubu Jorim)\*\*By \[Esther Choi\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZszgVG4-Lgc\&t=314s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 14 oz  Tofu, firm, cut into 1 inch thick squares&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Soy sauce, divided&#10;  - ½ Cup Hot water&#10;  - 6 Dried Shiitake Mushrooms&#10;  - 1 Onion, small, diced&#10;  - 2 Green onions, chopped&#10;  - 1 Red chili pepper, minced&#10;  - 2 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Tsp Ginger, minced&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Gochugaru&#10;  - 1 Tsp Paprika&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Toasted Sesame oil&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Mirin&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Toasted Sesame seeds      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Place tofu on a sheet tray and rub 1 Tbsp soy sauce over tofu to pre-season. Leave for 10-15 minutes.&#10;2.  Place shiitake mushrooms into a bowl with hot water and set aside.&#10;3.  Add onions to a cold pan with a little bit of vegetable oil. Slowly cook to caramelize. Take about 10 minutes.&#10;4.  To make marinade, add to a bowl green onions, pepper, garlic, ginger, 3 Tbsp soy sauce, sesame oil, gochugaru, paprika, sugar, mirin,  and sesame seeds.&#10;5.  Once mushrooms are soft, squeeze out the moisture into the bowl and set aside. Add mushroom liquid to the marinade bowl and mix.&#10;6.  Remove stems from mushrooms and slice.&#10;7.  Dry tofu with a paper towel.&#10;8.  Large pan with a lid, add vegetable oil. Heat on medium..&#10;9.  Add tofu to the pan. Flip to fry both sides.&#10;10. Lower heat to low, add onions and mushrooms to the top of the tofu.&#10;11. Top with sauce.&#10;12. Turn up heat to medium, once simmering, cover for 5-10 minutes. Cook until reduced and sauce is thick.&#10;13. Serve with rice.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Crispy Baked Tofu\*\*By \[Aaron and Claire\](https://youtu.be/OeqE\_HrKIAQ?t=201) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 17 oz (500 g) Tofu, firm&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Korean chili paste (Gochujang)&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Corn syrup, light&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Neutral oil&#10;  - Salt and Pepper to taste&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sesame oil, toasted&#10;  - Microgreens, Salad mix, or Green onions&#10;  - Toasted sesame seeds      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To make sauce, stir together Korean chili paste, corn syrup, soy sauce, oyster sauce, garlic, and oil.&#10;2.  Pat dry tofu with paper towels.&#10;3.  Cut into tofu like a checkered pattern, but not all the way. Cut about halfway down.&#10;4.  Season tofu with salt and pepper. Then place onto parchment paper.&#10;5.  Generously brush sauce onto tofu.&#10;6.  In an air fryer, cook at 200 degrees C (390 degrees F) for 15 minutes. Could try 20-25 minutes in the oven.&#10;7.  Drizzle with sesame oil and top off with microgreens and sesame seeds.&#10;8.  Can serve with white rice.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Mapo Tofu (Japanese)\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez Alt\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dElALuJ9Xo8) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 12 Oz Firm Silken Tofu, sliced into small cubes&#10;  - 12-16 Oz Ground beef&#10;  - 2 Stalks Green Onion, diced&#10;  - 3 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Nub Ginger, minced&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sake&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Mirin&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Shoyu or Light Soy Sauce&#10;  - 2 Tsp Cornstarch, with 2 tsp water to form slurry.&#10;  - Optional: Chicken stock&#10;  - Optional: Chili oil&#10;  - Chili crisp/garlic/oil      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pan on medium-high heat with oil.&#10;2.  Add beef and break apart until cooked.&#10;3.  Add garlic, ginger, and green onions. Cook for a few minutes.&#10;4.  Add sake, mirin, and soy sauce. Mix.&#10;5.  Add tofu. &#10;6.  Add some cornstarch slurry to thicken the sauce.  If too thick, add water. If too thin, add more slurry.&#10;7.  Could add some chili oil or chicken stock if preferred.&#10;8.  Top with chili crisp, chili garlic, or chili oil per preference.&#10;9.  Serve over rice.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Mapo Tofu (Cantonese)\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3XSXygYEJos) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 12 Oz Firm Silken Tofu, sliced into small cubes&#10;  - 3 Oz Ground pork&#10;  - 1 Stalk Green Onion, diced&#10;  - 3 Shallots, thinly sliced &#10;  - 2 Dried chili peppers, chopped&#10;  - 2 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Nub Ginger, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Chili Bean Sauce (Spicy Doubanjiang)&#10;      &#10;      - Alternatives: 1 Tbsp Ground bean sauce + 0.5 Tsp Chili sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Light Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Dark Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1.5 Tsp Cornstarch, with 2 tsp water to form slurry.&#10;  - 1 Tsp sesame oil&#10;  - Chili crisp/garlic/oil | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pan on medium-high heat with oil.&#10;2.  Add garlic, ginger, shallots, and dried chilis. Cook for 15-20 seconds.&#10;3.  Add pork and break apart until cooked.&#10;4.  Add chili bean sauce (Or Ground bean sauce).&#10;5.  Add 1 cup water and simmer on low.&#10;6.  Add light soy sauce, dark soy sauce, oyster sauce,  sugar, and salt. (Add chili sauce).&#10;7.  Add tofu, mix, and cover the pan for 1-2 minutes.&#10;8.  Add some cornstarch slurry to thicken the sauce.  If too thick, add water. If too thin, add more slurry.&#10;9.  Finish with green onions and sesame oil.&#10;10. Top with chili crisp, chili garlic, or chili oil per preference.&#10;11. Serve over rice. \*\*Notes\*\*Can add more dark soy sauce to darken the color.Optional to add sweet peppers when adding the green onions at step 9..    |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Coconut Tuna Dip (Lohn pla tuna)\*\*By Pailin’s Kitchen (Sabai) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 4oz Can Tuna, unsalted&#10;  - ½-¾ Cup Coconut milk&#10;  - ¼ Cup Shallots, julienned&#10;  - 2-3 Thai chilies, halved&#10;  - 3-inch Lemongrass, bottom half only, thinly sliced&#10;  - 2 Tsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 2 Tsp Tamarind paste&#10;  - 1 Tsp Palm sugar&#10;  - 3-4 Sprigs Cilantro, chopped     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a pot, boil coconut milk and shallots on medium heat. Boil for a few minutes until reduced by half.&#10;2.  Add tuna, thai chilies, lemon grass, fish sauce, tamarind paste, and sugar. Mix and break up chunks of tuna. Cook on low for 2 minutes.&#10;3.  If too thick, can up to ¼ cup of coconut milk.  Should have a dip like consistency.&#10;4.  Taste and adjust seasoning with fish sauce, tamarind paste, or sugar as needed.&#10;5.  Prior to serving, stir in cilantro. &#10;6.  Serve with crackers, dipping vegetables, crispy pork rind, or rice.  \*\*Notes\*\*  &#10;Can garnish top with more thai chilis cilantro, and a splash of coconut milk..    |

| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Red Curry Tuna Stir Fry\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dlROM9N5rak) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Cans Tuna, unsalted&#10;  - 3-4 Makrut lime leaves, thinly, could remove thick stems&#10;  - 1 Stalk Lemongrass, bottom half only, thinly sliced&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Red curry paste&#10;  - 1 Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - 1 Tsp Turmeric&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Palm sugar&#10;  - ? Tbsp Fish sauce     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a pan on medium low, add 2-3 Tbsp oil, curry paste, black pepper, turmeric, and palm sugar&#10;2.  Add tuna and stir fry until dry.&#10;3.  Add lime leaves and lemon grass. Toss briefly.&#10;4.  Taste and add fish sauce to taste.&#10;5.  Serve with rice. \*\*Notes\*\*  &#10;Can add thai chilis if preferred.   |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Tuna Sisig\*\*By \[Kuya Fern\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qrwMC\_5NN4A) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 1 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 3 4oz Cans Tuna, drained&#10;  - 2 Onions, minced&#10;  - 2-3 Green chili peppers, chipped&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Seasoning sauce&#10;  - 1 Pinch Salt&#10;  - 1 Pinch Black pepper&#10;  - 5-6 Tbsp Mayonnaise&#10;  - Calamansi     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add oil from 2 canned tuna to pan, on medium heat.&#10;2.  Add 1 onion and saute for 20 seconds.&#10;3.  Add tuna and saute.&#10;4.  Add salt and black pepper. Mix and saute until tuna is reduced.&#10;5.  Add green chili peppers and mix. Continue to reduce.&#10;6.  Once tuna is lightly toasted and oil is reduced. Turn off the flame and set aside.&#10;7.  To tuna, add raw onion, mayonnaise, soy sauce, and seasoning sauce. Mix. Could add chicharon to give crunch. Or crushed Kropek. Could serve on sizzling plate. Serve with a side of calamansi juice. \*\*Notes\*\*  &#10;Can add red chilis if preferred.   |

| \# \*\*Asparagus\*\*    |

| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Asparagus-muchim\*\*By \[Maangchi\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tsCPiwK\_Zm8) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 10 oz Asparagus&#10;  - 2 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 1 Stalk Green onion, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Apple cider vinegar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Gochujang&#10;  - 2 Tsp Sesame oil&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Salt&#10;  - Toasted sesame seeds&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Rice syrup&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Honey, Corn syrup, or Sugar      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Blanch asparagus in boiling water for 1-2 minutes, depending on thickness. Rinse in cold water.&#10;2.  Add all ingredients in a mixing bowl and mix.&#10;3.  Can top with more sesame seeds. \*\*Notes\*\*Prefer thin and long asparagus. Break off or cut off the tough woody bottom end of asparagus. Can use these ends for vegetable stock. Can peel if thick pieces.    |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Braised Asparagus\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=cPYDguwABAM) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 10 Stalks Asparagus&#10;  - ½ Cup Vegetable stock&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - Salt&#10;  - Black pepper      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Sear asparagus in neutral  oil or olive oil. Season with salt and pepper.&#10;2.  Sear until lightly browned per preference. Flip.&#10;3.  Add butter and vegetable stock and cover.&#10;4.  Cook for a few minutes. Uncover and continue to cook to reduce into a glaze. Should not be too watery, if it starts to look greasy, then you reduced too much. \*\*Notes\*\*Prefer thin and long asparagus. Break off or cut off the tough woody bottom end of asparagus. Can use these ends for vegetable stock. Can peel if thick pieces.    |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Avocado Cilantro Dressing\*\*By Hobby and Cook |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Avocado&#10;  - ½ Cup Sour cream or Greek yogurt&#10;  - 1 Cup Cilantro, minced&#10;  - 1-2 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Lemon juice or Lime juice      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Blend all ingredients in a food processor or a blender.&#10;2.  Add ½ cup water and blend again. \*\*Notes\*\*Store for up to 1 week.If you want it thicker, add less water.Could add some oil if preferred.Could add pepper for spice.Can add boiled or roasted onions and jalapeno to make a salsa.  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Avocado Korean Salad and Avocado Sashimi\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*\*\*For Korean Salad:\*\*  - ½  Avocado, cubed&#10;  - 1 Stalk Green onion, minced&#10;  - 2-3 Leaves Lettuce, thinly chopped&#10;  - 1 Clove, garlic minced or pressed&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Lemon juice&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Roasted sesame seeds\*\*For Sashimi:\*\*  - ½ Avocado, sliced&#10;  - Rice&#10;  - Soy sauce&#10;  - Wasabi      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  For Korean salad, mix all ingredients, adding lettuce last.&#10;2.  For Sashimi, serve with all ingredients. \*\*Notes\*\*  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Avocado Toast\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Avocado&#10;  - Sourdough bread      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Lightly season bread with salt and pepper. Add a touch of olive oil to both sides and toast both sides on a pan.&#10;2.  Place avocado slices onto bread and either flute out or crush per preference.&#10;3.  Top with preferred toppings. \*\*Toppings\*\*1\.  Kylie Jenner - honey, chili flakes, salt&#10;2.  Gordon Ramsay - lemon juice, black sesame seeds, chili flakes, lemon zest, salt, pepper&#10;3.  Joshua Weissman&#10;      &#10;      - Plain Jane - salt, pepper, olive oil&#10;      - New Yorker - smoked salmon, red onion thinly sliced, tomato slices,  capers&#10;      - Egg - soft boiled egg (6.5 minutes) cut in half, salt, parmesan cheese&#10;      - Samurai - togarashi powder, salt&#10;4.  Bobby Flay &#10;      &#10;      -  green onions, shallots, cilantro, lime juice, egg salad&#10;      - Green onions, cilantro, lime juice, smoked salmon&#10;5.  Lemon juice, salt, pepper, black sesame seeds, white sesame seeds, poppy seeds, garlic and onion dehydrated \*\*Notes\*\*Lemon juice prevents avocado from browning.   |

| :-: |
| \# \*\*Beans\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Refried Beans\*\*By \[Views on the Road\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=K\_l2RhftZQs) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Cups Pinto beans&#10;  - 1 White or yellow onion&#10;      &#10;      - ½ chopped into quarters&#10;      - ½ finely chopped&#10;  - 4-5 Garlic cloves, smashed&#10;  - ½ Jalapeno or serrano pepper, finely chopped&#10;  - 2 Tbsp salt      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Boil water in a pot and add the quarters of onion, garlic, salt, and pinto beans&#10;2.  Cook on low heat for 2-3 hours, until beans have a mushy soft consistency.&#10;3.  In a pan, heat oil and add finely chopped onions and peppers. Cook until onions are soft and translucent, but make sure to not burn.&#10;4.  Ladle beans in with juice and all. Smash the beans with a masher until preferred consistency. Add salt to taste. Can add cheese on top if preferred. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with \[Carnitas and Salsa\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.8rlfbmzgmv2v) or \[Al Pastor Tacos\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.boc2i89jf2fv).    |

  

|  |
| :-: |
| \# \*\*Bok Choy\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Bok Choy Pork Soup\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=cFbM5XGlfn4) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 5 oz Pork chops or Pork belly,, thinly sliced&#10;  - Bok choy, stems separated from leaves&#10;  - 1 Nub Ginger, sliced or minced&#10;  - 1 Tsp Cornstarch&#10;  - 1 Tsp oyster sauce&#10;  - 2 Cups Chicken broth&#10;  - 1 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1 Tsp Vegetable oil&#10;  - ½ Cube of Knorr Chicken powder or ½ Tsp Chicken bouillon&#10;  - 1 Stalk Green onion, sliced&#10;  - Black pepper        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Marinate meat with cornstarch, oyster sauce, and 1 Tbsp water.&#10;2.  On high heat, add 2 Cups water and chicken broth to a pot. &#10;3.  Add ginger, salt, and vegetable  oil to the pot.&#10;4.  Once boiling, add stalks first, then add leaves.&#10;5.  Cover with lid and return to gentle boil (Takes 3-5 minutes usually).&#10;6.  Add pork to the pot.&#10;7.  Add Knorr chicken powder or Chicken bouillon to the pot.&#10;8.  Cook for about 1-2 minutes until the soup is boiling again.&#10;9.  Can remove bok choy and pork from soup and serve separate from soup.&#10;10. Finish soup with green onion and black pepper. \*\*Notes\*\*For soups, use 3-4  outer leaves of bok choy. Use the inner leaves for stir fry as they are tougher. Store bok choy by wrapping the inside of the bag  with a paper towel and poke holes in the bag for longer storage up to a week.Can season with fish oil, soy sauce, sriracha, and sesame oil per preference.     |

| \# \*\*Brussels Sprouts\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Brussels Sprouts\*\*By ThatDudeCanCook |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 lb Brussels sprouts, trimmed and sliced vertically&#10;  -           | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Blanch brussels sprouts in boiling water with salt for 6-7 minutes.&#10;2.  Strain and let steam for 2 minutes.&#10;3.  Place in bowl with a bit olive oil and salt, toss.&#10;4.  On a sheet pan, add more olive oil, place sprouts flesh side down spaced apart.&#10;5.  Bake 400 degrees F for 25-30 minutes.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Maple Fish Sauce Brussels Sprouts\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QEAer9lK\_Yc) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 lb Brussel sprouts, trimmed and sliced vertically&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Maple syrup or honey&#10;  - 4 Tsp Fish sauce&#10;  - ¼ Tsp white pepper or black pepper&#10;  - Optional: \[Crispy Garlic\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.rdsjn5u6o4i) or Garlic powder&#10;  - Optional: \[Garlic Oil\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.rdsjn5u6o4i)        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add garlic oil or cooking oil to the pan.&#10;2.  Add brussels sprouts flat side down.&#10;3.  Heat on medium high until  well browned on the bottom side.&#10;4.  Then toss or flip the sprouts to cook on the other side. Cook for 1-2 minutes.&#10;5.  If there are extra sprouts to cook, turn off heat, remove brussels sprouts  and cook the second batch of sprouts.&#10;6.  Put all the sprouts back in the pan with heat off.&#10;7.  Add fish sauce, maple syrup, and pepper. Mix to coat evenly..&#10;8.  Can serve at room temperature or hot.&#10;9.  Optional to top with crispy garlic or garlic powder.      |

| :-: |
| \# \*\*Carrots\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Carrot Fries\*\*By Tasty |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\* 2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Carrots, chopped into strips&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Oil&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Garlic powder&#10;  - 1 Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  - ¼ Cup Grated Parmesan        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Toss all ingredients on a baking sheet and bake at 400 degrees F for 15-20 minutes. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with \[Greek Yogurt Dip\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.9ty2rr57zlm9) or \[Cheese Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.7e8z0kq4ryrw).Could toss with cumin, parsley, paprika.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Creamy Carrot Soup\*\*By The Stay At Home Chef and RecipeTin |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*6 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 5 Carrots, chopped&#10;  - 1 Onion, chopped&#10;  - 2 Cloves Garlic, chopped&#10;  - 4 Cups Vegetable Broth&#10;  - 2 Tsp Red Thai Chili Paste&#10;  - 1 Cup Heavy cream        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Saute onions and garlic in pot with butter/oil for about 5 minutes until onions are soft and turning translucent. Season onions with salt.&#10;2.  Add thai red curry paste and cook for about 1 minute.&#10;3.  Add in carrots and vegetable broth.&#10;4.  Once boiling, reduce to medium low and simmer for about 20 minutes until carrots are tender.&#10;5.  Puree soup via blender or use an immersion blender until smooth.&#10;6.  Pour in heavy cream and stir.&#10;7.  Season with salt and pepper. \*\*Notes\*\*Could top with thyme, pepper, parsley, sour cream, bacon, or dill.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Vietnamese Pickled Carrots\*\*By Gaming Foodie |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1 Lb Carrots, sliced&#10;  - 1 ¼ Cup Vinegar&#10;  - 1 Cup hot water&#10;  - 1 Cup sugar&#10;  - 1.5 Tbsp Salt        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cover carrots with sugar and salt, mix, and rest for at least 5 minutes (Longer for thicker slices). Carrots are ready when bendable without snapping.&#10;2.  Boil water with vinegar, sugar, and salt. &#10;3.  Add hot mixture and carrots to a jar. \*\*Notes\*\*Could try adding cumin or serrano peppers.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Vietnamese Pickled Carrots and Daikon\*\*By Tasty Like Whoa |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Medium sized carrots, sliced into matchsticks&#10;  - ½ Daikon, sliced into matchsticks&#10;  - Sugar&#10;  - Salt        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Place carrots and daikon into bowl. Add 1 Tbsp Sugar and mix and let for a couple of minutes . ½ Tbsp salt for a minute. Let sit for 5 minute until bendable. Wash for 3 minutes.&#10;2.  Shake off excess water and squeeze.&#10;3.  Into a container.&#10;4.  3 Cups of warm water into bowl. 4 Tbsp sugar. 5 Tbsp rice vinegar or distilled vinegar. Mix until dissolved. Add sliced thai chilis per preference. Add to daikon and carrots. &#10;5.  Store for 2-3 days. Good in the fridge for up to 1 month. \*\*Notes\*\*Could try adding cumin or serrano peppers.      |

| \# \*\*Cauliflower\*\*    |

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Roasted Cauliflower\*\*Modified recipe by\[ Ina\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4cnbxsN8oz8) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Cauliflower head, cut into bite size florets&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Olive oil&#10;  - Kosher salt and freshly ground black pepper&#10;  - 1 Cup Parmesan cheese, freshly grated&#10;  - Honey        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Place cauliflower onto a sheet pan and drizzle with olive oil and sprinkle generously with salt and pepper. Toss well.&#10;2.  Bake in oven at 350 degrees F for 30 minutes until tender and starts to brown. Toss once at 15 minute mark.&#10;3.  Sprinkle with parmesan cheese and honey and bake for 2 more minutes until cheese melts.&#10;4.  Season to taste.&#10;5.  Can top with chopped parsley           |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Spanish Garlic Cauliflower\*\*By \[Spain on a Fork\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=T7bN3f70B44) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Cauliflower head, cut into bite size florets (\\\~25 florets)&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Olive oil, extra virgin&#10;  - 6 Garlic cloves, roughly chopped&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sweet smoked Spanish paprika&#10;  - Handful of fresh parsley&#10;  - Pinch of Sea salt&#10;  - Dash of Black pepper       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pot with water and salt until boiling.&#10;2.  Add cauliflower florets and cook for 4-6 minutes. Cauliflower can be easily pierced with a toothpick. &#10;3.  Drain cauliflower and set aside.&#10;4.  Heat pan on low-medium heat with olive oil. &#10;5.  Add garlic and cook for 1 minute. &#10;6.  Add paprika, mix, then add florets. &#10;7.  Season with salt and pepper. Mix to coat.&#10;8.  Transfer to serving dish and sprinkle with parsley.           |

  

|  |
| :-: |
| \# \*\*Corn\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Corn Chowder\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez\](https://www.seriouseats.com/the-food-lab-life-the-best-corn-chowder) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Serves 3 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2-3 Husks Corn, kennels sliced off and scraped off, cobs cut in half&#10;  - 1 Russet Potato or Yukon gold potato, peeled and cut into small cubes&#10;  - 3 Stalked Green onions, minced&#10;  - 1 Onion or Leek, minced&#10;  - 1-2 Stalks Celery, minced&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - 3 Cups Chicken stock or Vegetable stock&#10;  - 2 Cloves, Garlic&#10;  - 1 Tsp Coriander seeds&#10;  - 1 Tsp Black peppercorns&#10;  - 1 Tsp Fennel seeds&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Flour&#10;  - 2 Cups Whole Milk or Half and Half&#10;  - 2 Bay leaves&#10;  - Salt, pepper, sugar&#10;  - Optional: 4 Slices Bacon, cut into small dice | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To a pot, add corn cobs, chicken stock, bay leaves, coriander seeds, black peppercorns, and fennel seeds. Bring to a boil, then lower heat and simmer for 10 minutes. Strain and discard cobs and spices. Set aside corn stock.&#10;2.  If using bacon, cook pot on medium heat until water evaporates, pork begins to brown and crisp in spots (7-10 minutes). Remove half and place onto a paper towel. Then move on to step 3.&#10;3.  Heat butter in a pot on moderate heat. Add corn kernels, celery, onions, and garlic. Mix for 7 minutes until corn kernels are soft.&#10;4.  Add flour to the pot and mix for 1 minute..&#10;5.  Stir while slowly adding corn stock.&#10;6.  Add potatoes and 1 Tsp salt.&#10;7.  Stir occasionally and simmer for 10 minutes, until potatoes are tender.&#10;8.  Add milk and stir.&#10;9.  Can take ¼ of soup and blend, or use a hand blender in soup until preferred consistency.&#10;10. Season with salt, pepper, and sugar.&#10;11. Top with green onions. \*\*Notes\*\*Once chilled, can whisk in water 1 tbsp at a time until preferred consistency.Younger corn does not require as much sugar added.     |

| \# \*\*Cucumbers\*\*    |

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Dill Pickles\*\* By \[Joshua Weissman\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NUremX6K\_4Y) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Cucumbers, sliced&#10;  - 2 Cups water&#10;  - 1.5 Cups White distilled vinegar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Kosher salt&#10;  - 2 Bay Leaves, crushed&#10;  - 1 Pinch Turmeric&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Black peppercorn&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Mustard seeds&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Coriander seeds&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Dill seed or weed&#10;  - 1 Bunch Fresh Dill&#10;  - 4 Cloves Garlic, smashed        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add cucumbers to a jar.&#10;2.  Add peppercorn, mustard seeds, coriander seeds, and dill seed/weed to a cheese cloth to make a satchel.&#10;3.  Add water, vinegar, salt, bay leaves, turmeric, and satchel to a pot, then bring to a boil.&#10;4.  Pour hot mixture into jar with cucumbers. &#10;5.  Add fresh dill and garlic to jar.&#10;6.  Allow to cool. Store in the refrigerator. \*\*Notes\*\*Can be used with \[Hamburgers\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.vhtfd374new7).      |

| :-: | :-: |

Add vegetables to the jar.&#10;2.  To a pot, add soy sauce, vinegar, sugar, and water. Cook until boiling and boil for 10 seconds.&#10;3.  Turn off heat and pour hot mixture into the jar. Do not fill all the way to the top as water can come out of vegetables.&#10;4.  Let sit and cool and store in the refrigerator over night.l. \*\*Notes\*\*Could try adding cumin to pickling jar.      |

| :-: | :-: |

Whisk sugar, vinegar, salt, and oil into a mixing bowl. Thenn add the rest of the ingredients and toss. Marinate in the refrigerator for at least 1 hour. Can store for 2-3 days.        |

Cover cucumbers with sugar and salt, mix, and rest for at least 20 minutes, until they can bend without snapping. Drain out excess water.&#10;2.  Boil water with vinegar, sugar, and salt. &#10;3. | :-: | :-: |

Cover cucumbers with 3 tsp salt, mix, and rest for at least 20 minutes, until they can bend without snapping. Drain out excess water.&#10;2.  Boil water with vinegar and sugar.&#10;3. | :-: |
| \# \*\*Daikon/Korean Radish\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Braised Daikon\*\*By Lucas Sin |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Daikon, peeled and sliced into 1-2 inch chunks&#10;  - 1-2 Nubs Ginger, smashed&#10;  - 4 Cloves Garlic, smashed&#10;  - 2 Stalks Green onions, sliced into 2 inch pieces&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Sichuan peppercorns&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Light soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Dark soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Seasoning soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - ½ Tbsp sugar         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Simmer daikon in pot with 1 Tbsp of rice or some rice water (Optional). Cover daikon by ½-1 inch of water. Simmer for at least 30 minutes. The rice helps remove the daikon smell. Can taste liquid which is bitter. Chipstick should easily go into daikon and it can slide off.&#10;2.  Take out daikon. Empty pot.&#10;3.  In pot with oil on medium, add ginger, garlic, green onion, and sichuan peppercorns until fragrant.&#10;4.  Carefully add cold water (2 Cups) into pot, add sauces. &#10;5.  Once boiling, add daikon and simmer for 30 minutes uncovered. \*\*Notes\*\*      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Daikon Steak\*\*By Maanchi |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Daikon, 3-4x 1.5-2 inch circular slices pieces&#10;  - Chicken bouillon/Chicken stock&#10;  - Hondashi&#10;  - 2 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  -            | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Peel skin of daikon and save skins. Score top and bottom of daikon with knife. Can peel to curve edges.&#10;2.  Salt the daikon.&#10;3.  To a bowl, add the daikin skins, chicken bouillon, hondashi 0.85 Cups water. Cover with paper towel.&#10;4.  Microwave for 10 minutes with 600w microwave.&#10;5.  Remove daikon and filter liquid for use later.&#10;6.  Make garlic oil and filter out garlic crips.&#10;7.  Cook daikon in a Tbsp of garlic oil until browned on bot sides.&#10;8.  Add a bit of butter.&#10;9.  Add some of broth prepared earlier. Cook to boil, cover and cook for 3-4 minutes.&#10;10. Can flip and baste.&#10;11. Add garlic chips.&#10;12. Plate and top with parsley, black pepper,  and the sauce in pan. \*\*Notes\*\*Can cook in pot instead of microwave.     |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Pickled Radish\*\*By \[Maanchi\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=82XKHyJ4jak) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 lb Daikon or Korean radish, peeled and cut into small ½ inch cubes&#10;  - ⅓ Cup White sugar&#10;  - ⅓ Cup + 1 Tbsp white vinegar&#10;  - 1 Tsp Salt&#10;  - ¾ Cup Water         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add sugar, vinegar, salt, and water to mixing bowl and mix to dissolve.&#10;2.  Add radish, mix, and store in container.&#10;3.  Refrigerate for a couple of hours. Taste better after 12 hours. \*\*Notes\*\*      |

  

|  |  |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Radish Rice (Mu-bap)\*\*By \[Maanchi\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VV4wAJadyLA\&t=316s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 lb Korean radish, sliced into matchsticks&#10;  - 1 Cup Rice\*\*For Sauce:\*\*  - 4 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 3 Stalks Green onions, minced&#10;  - ¼ Cup Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Gochugaru&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Toasted sesame seeds, coarsely ground&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sesame oil         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Rinse rice in  water and soak for 30 minutes.&#10;2.  In rice cooker, put half of radish on bottom, then add rice. Add 1 cup water. Top with left over radish. Cook.&#10;3.  Prepare sauce by mixing ingredients. &#10;4.  Mix rice and radish.&#10;5.  When serving, can add in sauce to a bowl of rice and mix as preferred. \*\*Notes\*\*Can eat with mushroom bulgogi.     |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Boiled Soy Sauce Mushrooms\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Mushrooms, sliced thinly&#10;  - Light Soy sauce       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pot with water until boiling.&#10;2.  Add mushrooms and reduce to medium heat.&#10;3.  Cook until all water is evaporated.&#10;4.  Add some neutral oil and saute for 2-3 minutes.&#10;5.  Add soy sauce and saute for 1 minute.  \*\*Notes\*\*After step 3, boiled mushrooms can be used in the \[Stir Fry Sauce\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.bs9zzhg0i0pi) recipe.           |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Bulgogi King Oyster Mushrooms\*\*By \[Maangchi\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=srHw4xQj1I0\&t=279s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Large King Oyster Mushrooms, thinly sliced&#10;  - 4 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 3 Stalks Green onions, minced&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 5 Tbsp Water&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Toasted Sesame oil&#10;  - 1.5 Tbsp Toasted sesame seeds, coarsely ground&#10;  - 1 Tsp Black pepper      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Combine all ingredients and mix. Can marinate if preferred.&#10;2.  Grill mushrooms until cooked on both sides until soft. While cooking, pour the extra sauce over. \*\*Notes\*\*            |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Garlic Butter Mushrooms\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Neutral oil&#10;  - 12 oz Mushrooms (Shiitake, button, or cremini), trimmed and cut into quarters&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Mirin or White wine&#10;  - 2 Tsp Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - Thyme, minced&#10;  - Parsley, minced&#10;  - Kosher salt and freshly ground black pepper       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pan on high heat and oil.&#10;2.  Add mushrooms and stir-fry until deeply browned and crisp in spots, but still juicy and tender. About 5 minutes. Reduce heat if mushrooms threaten to burn. Add butter in the middle of cooking.&#10;3.  Add garlic and cook until fragrant. About 15 seconds.&#10;4.  Add thyme and parsley&#10;5.  Add Mirin and toss to combine.&#10;6.  Season with salt and pepper.&#10;7.   Toss until mushrooms are thoroughly coated.&#10;8.  Season to taste with salt and pepper.              |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Soy-Glazed Mushrooms\*\*By Kenji-Lopez Alt |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Tbsp Neutral oil&#10;  - 12 oz Mushrooms (Shiitake, button, or cremini), trimmed and cut into quarters&#10;  - 2 Tsp Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - 2 Tsp Ginger, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Light soy sauce or shoyu&#10;  - 2 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tsp Roasted sesame oil&#10;  - 2 Stalks Green onion, thinly sliced&#10;  - 2 Tsp Toasted sesame seeds&#10;  - Kosher salt and freshly ground black pepper&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - Optional: 4 Dried chili peppers  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pan on high heat and add oil.&#10;2.  Add mushrooms and stir-fry until deeply browned and crisp in spots, but still juicy and tender. About 5 minutes. Reduce heat if mushrooms threaten to burn.&#10;3.  Add garlic and ginger (and optional chili peppers), and cook until fragrant. About 15 seconds.&#10;4.  Add soy sauce (and optional oyster sauce) and toss to combine.&#10;5.  Add sugar, sesame oil, green onions, and sesame seeds. Toss until mushrooms are thoroughly coated.&#10;6.  Season to taste with salt and pepper. \*\*Notes\*\*Can be stored in the refrigerator for up to a week.Can be added to salads, soups, or other stir fries. \*\*Add chicken:\*\*1-2 Chicken breasts/thighs, sliced into bite size pieces.Prior to step 1, cook chicken on medium heat with oil until golden. Then cover and cook on low heat for 3 minutes. Set aside and add chicken after step 2.  Increase the amount of ingredients in next steps  to match serving. Can add 1 Tbsp cornstarch + 2 Tbsp water slurry to make the sauce more thick. \*\*Add Bok choy:\*\*Prior to step 1, add water to the pan on medium and add bok choy.  Cover for a minute. Stir and cook for another minute.  Set aside, and re add after step 3. Can add 1 Tbsp cornstarch + 2 Tbsp water slurry to make the sauce more thick.      |

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Blanched Chinese Lettuce\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez-Alt\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0tzLmt7Zp4\&t=276s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Head Romaine Lettuce, head sliced off&#10;  - 4-5 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - Salt&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add \\\~5 Cups water  to a pot and heat to boil. Add pinch of salt.&#10;2.  Rinse lettuce.&#10;3.  To make sauce, add 1 Tbsp oil to a pan on high heat. &#10;4.  Add garlic to the pan and saute for 2 minutes. &#10;5.  To the pan, add a pinch of salt, oyster sauce, soy sauce, and 3 Tbsp of water. Mix to make sauce and turn off heat.&#10;6.  Blanch romaine lettuce in boiling water for 20 seconds. This wilts the lettuce while retaining crispness.&#10;7.  Plate lettuce and pour sauce over. &#10;8.  Serve right away to avoid lettuce getting too soft.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Korean Lettuce Salad\*\*By \[Maangchi\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=umRqugilSkA\&t=57s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Head Romaine Lettuce&#10;  - 2 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - 1 Green onion, chopped&#10;  - ¼ Cup onion, thinly sliced&#10;  - 4 Tbsp soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tsp fish sauce (or salt)&#10;  - 2 Tsp sugar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp white vinegar&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Korean hot pepper flakes/Gochugaru (or 1 Tbsp Gochujang/chili paste)&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Toasted sesame oil&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Toasted sesame seeds      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Wash and drain lettuce. Shake lettuce to remove excess water.&#10;2.  Tear lettuce into bite size pieces and set aside.&#10;3.  Combine rest of ingredients into large mixing bowl. Mix well until sugar is dissolved.&#10;4.  Can quickly cook green onion and onion in a pan prior to adding in if preferred.&#10;5.  Add lettuce and mix gently to coat.&#10;6.  Sprinkle with sesame seeds and serve right away as a side dish for rice.       |

| \# \*\*Bread\*\*    |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*No Knead Bread in Dutch Oven\*\*By Liza S. |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 3 Cups all purpose flour (Sifted)&#10;  - 1 Tsp salt&#10;  - ¾ Tsp instant yeast&#10;  - 1 ½ Cups warm (Not hot) water       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Mix warm water with yeast. Set aside for 5 minutes. Expect bubbles to form if yeast is good.&#10;2.  Mix flour and salt. Add yeasty water. Mix well with spatula.&#10;3.  Leave dough in bowl and cover with plastic wrap. Let sit for 8-12 hours at room temperature (Not to exceed 18 hours). It will become bubbly and will increase in size.&#10;4.  Transfer dough on a well-floured surface. Sprinkle some flour on the dough. Using scraper, fold dough over a couple of times. Line bowl with parchment paper and put the dough back. Slit lines on top of the dough (Can make designs). Cover and let sit white preheating oven.&#10;5.  Preheat oven to 450C&#10;6.  When oven is ready, move the parchment paper with the dough into dutch oven.&#10;7.  Bake covered for 30 minutes.&#10;8.  Remove paper then continue baking uncovered for 10-15 minutes to make crust crispy.&#10;9.  Cool on a rack to let the middle of the break bake completely.        |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Pandesal\*\*By \[Savor Easy\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4RiNlBSRi7w) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Cup Milk, warm&#10;  - ¼ Cup Butter&#10;  - 1 Tsp salt&#10;  - 2 Tsp (6 g) Yeast, instant&#10;  - 3 ¼ Cup Flour, all purpose&#10;  - ¼ Cup sugar&#10;  - Bread crumbs, fine       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Into a mixing bowl, add milk, butter, egg, and yeast. Mix ingredients a little.&#10;2.  Add flour, sugar, and salt. Mix until combined.&#10;3.  Let rest uncovered for 20 minutes.&#10;4.  Sprinkle some flour onto a clean surface and transfer dough to it.&#10;5.  Knead until dough is smooth for about 7 minutes.&#10;6.  Form into a ball and place into an oiled bowl. Get some oil on the ball.&#10;7.  Cover with saran wrap for an hour or until dough doubles in size.&#10;8.  Push onto dough to remove gas, then place onto surface and cut into 18 pieces.&#10;9.  Form each into a ball, pinch the seams to seal.&#10;10. Roll under palm to smoothen balls.&#10;11. Dredge balls into bread crumbs and place onto baking sheet.&#10;12. Cover with saran wrap and let rest in a warm place until doubled in size.&#10;13. Bake at 180 degrees C (350 degrees F) for 18-20 minutes. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with sugar and butter, toasted.      |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Garlic Fried Rice\*\*By \[Gideon General\](https://www.tiktok.com/@gidsgids/video/7119501053833301291?is\_copy\_url=1\&is\_from\_webapp=v1\&lang=en) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 5-10 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - 1-3 Cups cooked rice, 1 day old&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Neutral oil (Canola)&#10;  - Salt & Pepper&#10;  - Garlic powder      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cook pan on medium heat with oil.&#10;2.  Add garlic and saute for 1 minute.&#10;3.  Steam rice with some water in the microwave. Ensure rice is not stuck together.&#10;4.  Add rice to the pan and mix.&#10;5.  Add salt, pepper, and garlic powder to taste and mix.&#10;6.  Saute for about 3 minutes. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with \[Tocino\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.pu97mhjcjizt).      |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Ginger Egg Fried Rice\*\*By \[Cooking with Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FBScXlydaa8\&t=464s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 3 eggs&#10;  - 2 Cup cooked rice&#10;  - 0.5 Chicken breast (Alternatives: shrimp, pork, carrots, radishes, broccoli, corn)&#10;  - Salt&#10;  - Cornstarch&#10;  - 2 Nubs ginger, minced&#10;  - 2 Green onions, minced&#10;  - 1 Tsp salt  (or 1 Tbsp soy sauce, oyster sauce, chicken stock, chicken bouillon powder, to taste)&#10;  - 1 Tbsp neutral tasting oil&#10;  - 1-2 Green onions, sliced       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cook rice.&#10;2.  Cut chicken into thin strips, then into small bits. Marinate with 0.5 Tsp salt, 1 Tsp cornstarch, 2 Tbsp water. Mix and massage until marinade is fully absorbed, then mince.&#10;3.  Add ½ tsp salt to eggs, mix eggs.&#10;4.  Heat pan on high, then add 1-2 Tbsp oil.&#10;5.  Add eggs to the pan and constantly stir for 45-50 seconds. Nice soft consistency, place eggs on the side for later.&#10;6.  Add another 1-2 Tbsp of oil. Cook chicken in the pan for about 60 seconds.&#10;7.  Add ginger and stir fry for about 1 minute.&#10;8.  Add rice and cook for about 60 seconds. Spreading rice.&#10;9.  Add salt and stir.&#10;10. Add egg and mix for about 2 minutes.&#10;11. Add green onion and mix. \*\*Notes\*\*Can prepare without ginger to make Egg Fried Rice. Chicken is optional.        |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Kimchi Fried Rice\*\* |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Cup Kimchi, cut into bite sized pieces&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Neutral oil&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Korean chili pepper flakes (Gochugaru)&#10;  - ½ Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Cups cooked rice, 1 day old&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - 1-2 Green onion stalks, thinly sliced&#10;  - Sesame seeds&#10;  - Optional: 1 slice bacon, or thin beef, finely chopped&#10;  - Optional: ¼ onion, thinly sliced       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat pan on medium heat with oil.&#10;2.  Add bacon or beef and cook for 30 seconds.&#10;3.  Add green onions, push everything to one side and into empty space add soy sauce. Wait for a bit and then mix all together.&#10;4.  Add kimchi and stir to combine.&#10;5.  Add Korean chili pepper flakes and mix.&#10;6.  Add sugar and stir fry for at least 3 minutes.&#10;7.  Steam rice with some water in the microwave. Ensure rice is not stuck together.&#10;8.  Add rice and mix everything together. Make sure not to burn it by either turning off heat or add 1 Tbsp of water.&#10;9.  Add cooked egg on top. Top with sesame seeds and more green onions.       |

| :-: | :-: |

Coat rice with spices.&#10;2.  Heat pan on medium heat with oil.&#10;3.  Add potatoes and cook until golden color for about 5 minutes.&#10;4.  Add onions and salt.&#10;5.  Add garlic and dried chili.&#10;6.  Add rice.       |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Mushroom Fried Rice\*\* |  |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Kimchi Jeon (Fritter)\*\*By \[Doobdobap\](https://youtu.be/602kd5697OI?si=BBUJz7gbXLeZOutx\&t=476) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*1 Pancake \*\*Ingredients\*\*   - ¾ Cup Kimchi &#10;  - ¼ Cup Flour, All-purpose&#10;  - ¼ Cup Corn Starch &#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt &#10;  - ½ Tsp Gochugaru&#10;  - ½ Cup Water, Cold   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add all dry ingredients to a mixing bowl and mix.&#10;2.  Add water little by little without overmixing. Consistency should be very loose and resemble vomit.&#10;3.  If using a stainless steel pan, heat the pan until very hot and add 3 Tbsp oil to well coat.&#10;4.  Add batter and flatten.&#10;5.  Once the edges are cooked, flip the fritter.&#10;6.  Cook until both sides are done. \*\*Notes\*\*\*\*Serve with sauce:\*\*  - 1 Tbsp soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Vinegar&#10;  - Sesame seeds&#10;  - Hot pepper flakes&#10;  - Green onions  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Scallion Pancakes\*\*By\[ Inga Lam\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JzHX4JKsblM) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 ½ Cups Flour&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tsp Salt&#10;  - Scallions/Green onions, thinly sliced\*\*For Roux\*\*  - ½ Cup Flour&#10;  - ½ Cup Oil  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add flour to a mixing bowl and add ½ cup of hot water. Mix.&#10;2.  Add in ¼ cup room temperature water. Mix.&#10;3.  Add in sugar and salt. Mix.&#10;4.  Knead dough until no more flour on side of bowl. Then knead on a cutting board for at least 100 times. Cover with a towel and let rest for 20-30 minutes.&#10;5.  Slice scallions, using mainly green portions.&#10;6.  To make roux, in a pan add flour and oil on medium heat, stir to combine. Cook until mixed.&#10;7.  Dust flour onto cutting board. Roll dough into cylinder and divide per preference (4-5 pieces).&#10;8.  Roll dough until thin. Paint on the roux onto dough. Cover with scallions.&#10;9.  Can add 5-spice powder here.&#10;10. Roll dough over to cover scallions in. Then roll dough into itself again to form a pinwheel. Pinch dough together. Continue with other pieces. &#10;11. Roll dough out as much as possible.&#10;12. Add generous amount of oil into pan and add flattened dough.&#10;13. Fry until golden brown. Then flip. Can squish while cooking to add fluff. \*\*Notes\*\*Can store flattened dough in the freezer.  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Scallion Pancakes\*\*By\[ \](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JzHX4JKsblM)\[Ning Kang\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CCl4vgq1zYU) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Pancakes \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Cups Flour&#10;  - 1/2 Tsp Kosher Salt&#10;  - 3 Tsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 4 Scallions/Green onions, thinly sliced&#10;  - ¾ Tsp Chinese five-spice&#10;  - ¾ Tsp MSG&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Vegetable oil  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add flour to a mixing bowl and add ½ cup of hot water. Mix. &#10;2.  Add in 1/2 cup room temperature water. Mix. If dough is too sticky, add a Tbsp of flour. Let rest for up to 10 minutes, covered. Divide dough into 2 pieces.&#10;3.  Roll dough on a flour covered surface. Roll into 8-inch circle.&#10;4.  Sprinkle with salt, MSG, and five spice. Roll with rolling pin so spices stick to dough.&#10;5.  Drizzle with sesame oil and sprink;e with scallions.&#10;6.  Roll dough into a log, flatten slightly, and coil into a circle. Roll out into 8 inch circle again.&#10;7.  Repeat with other dough.&#10;8.  Heat 2 Tbsp oil on medium. Cook pancake, covered and flipping once until golden and crispy. 4-5 minutes per pancake. Repeat with other pancake.&#10;9.  Can mix sweet bean paste and yellow bean paste for sauce.&#10;10. Cut into wedges and serve. \*\*Notes\*\*Can store flattened dough in the freezer.  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*French Toast\*\*By\[ \](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JzHX4JKsblM)Kenji Lopez-Alt |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Pancakes \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 4 Slices bread&#10;  - 2 Eggs&#10;  - 1 Cup Milk or Half and half&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp Vanilla extract  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add eggs, milk, sugar, and vanilla extract to mixing bowl, and mix with whisk.&#10;2.  Soak bread slice into custard mixture. Can let sit in custard for a while.&#10;3.  Add salted butter to griddle or pan and heat.&#10;4.  Add toast.&#10;5.  Can spoon over some custard onto bread. &#10;6.  Cook at least 4 minutes per side.&#10;7.  Serve with butter and syrup. Could add cinnamon or powdered sugar. \*\*Notes\*\*For a crispier crust, can add sprinkle of sugar on one side before flipping.  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Buttermilk Pancakes\*\*By\[ \](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JzHX4JKsblM)Kenji Lopez-Alt |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Pancakes \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 ⅓ Cup Flour, all-purpose&#10;  - 1 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Baking powder&#10;  - 1 Cup Buttermilk, room temp&#10;  - 2 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 Egg&#10;  - 4 Tbsp Butter, melted | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a mixing bowl, add flour, salt, sugar, and baking powder.&#10;2.  In another container mix buttermilk, vanilla extract, egg, and butter. Add to dry ingredients and mix until just combined. Do not overmix.&#10;3.  Butter the pan on medium.&#10;4.  Add batter to pan and cook batter. \*\*Notes\*\*For a crispier crust, can add sprinkle of sugar on one side before flipping. 1 Egg, 1.5 Cups whole milk, whisk. 2 Cups all purpose flour. ¼ Cup sugar, 1 Tsp salt, ¼ Tsp Baking soda, 1 Tbsp Baking powder2 Tbsp melted butterCook 3-4 min.  |

| \# \*\*Potatoes\*\* |
|  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Crispy Oven Baked French Fries\*\*By \[Ethan Chlebowski\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dklh4oKifVQ) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Potato, peeled (Russet or Yukon gold)&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Vinegar&#10;  - 2 Pinch Salt&#10;  - 1/4 Cup Olive oil        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Set pot of water to boil.&#10;2.  Preheat oven to 450 degrees.&#10;3.  Peel and slice potato. First square off potato, then slice into about ½ inch sheets and then into ½ inch fries, or preference.&#10;4.  Add vinegar and salt to boiling water.&#10;5.  Add potatoes to pot and cook for 8 minutes with occasional stirring.&#10;6.  Strain fries into strainer.&#10;7.  Add to baking sheet and pour olive oil over potatoes. Mix to coat.&#10;8.  Add baking sheet to oven and cook for about 35 minutes. Flipping halfway through. Can cook longer if not crispy.&#10;9.  Add fries to a bowl with paper towel and add a pinch of salt. Mix then remove paper towel.&#10;10. Could season with preferred seasoning.            |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Chili Garlic Noodles\*\*By \[Joshua Weissman\](https://youtu.be/S-TmmjEN-V0?si=vSlkgZb\_Cu9noh1w\&t=577) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Noodles (Egg noodles, ramen noodles, wheat noodles, any asian)&#10;  - ⅓ Cup Vegetable oil&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Chili flakes&#10;  - 4 Cloves Garlic, grated&#10;  - ½ Tsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp MSG&#10;  - 2 Stalks Green onions, minced&#10;  - 2 ½ Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp White distilled vinegar&#10;  - 2 Tsp Sesame seeds, lightly ground     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat oil to 350 degrees F.&#10;2.  To a heat resistance bowl, add the rest of the ingredients. Pour hot oil over mixture.&#10;3.  Boil noodle according to directions. Drain and toss with chili oill.&#10;4.  Top with green onions, \*\*Notes\*\*Could serve with protein, such as tofu or chicken.            |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Filipino Spaghetti\*\*Modified recipe by Otakoyakisoba and Chef RV Manabet |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 6 oz/170 g Spaghetti noodles&#10;  - ½ lb/187.5 g Ground Beef&#10;  - 4-5 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 0.5  White Onion, chopped&#10;  - 4 Pcs Hotdogs or 1 can vienna sausage or spam, sliced&#10;  - 4 Mushrooms, sliced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1-2 Tbsp Tomato paste&#10;  - 1 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1 Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 1½ Cup Tomato/Pasta Sauce&#10;  - ½ Cups Jufran/Banana Catsup&#10;  - Sharp cheese, grated&#10;  - Salt, Black pepper, Sugar, to taste     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Boil 4 cups of water in a pot at high, add 1 Tbsp salt. Boil for 11 minutes until soft. Pour into a colander and save pasta water. Can wash pasta to stop it from cooking further.&#10;2.  Cook mushrooms on high medium heat with oil for a few minutes, then set aside.&#10;3.  On medium heat, cook olive oil. &#10;4.  Add onions and garlic to the pan.&#10;5.  Add beef, tomato paste and oyster sauce&#10;6.  Cook until meat is cooked, 5-10 minutes,&#10;7.  Add sugar, salt, black pepper. Can add sugar, salt, pepper here.&#10;8.  Add mushrooms, hotdogs, tomato/pasta sauce, and Jufran.&#10;9.  Mix and cook until boiling. Let simmer for 3-5 minutes. Can thicken with pasta water if needed.&#10;10. Add cheese, mix, and cook for 1-2 minutes. &#10;11. Serve cooked sauce over noodles.            |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Gochujang Noodle Soup\*\*By \[Aaron and Claire\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fmcmalhERAA\&t=484s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Onion, sliced&#10;  - Zucchini, thinly sliced&#10;  - Shitake mushroom, sliced&#10;  - Potato, thinly sliced&#10;  - 150g Ground beef&#10;  - Noodles&#10;  - 3 Green onion stalks, thinly sliced&#10;  - Gochujang        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  On medium heat, cook oil. &#10;2.  Add beef and saute for 1-2 minutes, until no longer pink.&#10;3.  Add ½ Tbsp soy sauce and 1 Tsp sugar, a few shakes of black pepper. Mix and cook for 1 minute.&#10;4.  Reduce to low heat, add green onion whites, 1 Tbsp minced garlic, 3 Tbsp Gochujang, 1 Tbsp korean soy bean paste, 1 Tbsp gochugaru. Stir fry for 3 minutes.&#10;5.  Once you can see some chili oil, add 6 cups of water and bring to a boil on medium-high heat.&#10;6.  Then add potatoes and mushrooms. Add 1 Tbsp chicken bouillon or beef bouillon.&#10;7.  Add ¼ Tsp MSG.&#10;8.  Add onion and noodles or follow package instructions.&#10;9.  Add zucchini at the end. &#10;10. Can add spicy chili pepper at the end. &#10;11. Can top with green onions, sesame seeds, and toasted seaweed.            |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Shanghai-Style Noodles with Scallion Oil\*\* By \[Kenji Lopez-Alt\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=lC87cKrwU5I) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*1-2 \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Portions of Noodles (Fresh wheat, spaghetti, or dry wheat)&#10;  - ¼ Cup \[Shanghai-Style Scallion Oil\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.si4tym2i58o5)&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Light soy sauce or shoyu&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tbsp Dark soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tsp Sugar        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cook noodles according to package directions.&#10;2.  Rinse the noodles briefly under cold water when they are done to stop cooking.&#10;3.  To a pan, add the scallion oil, light and dark soy sauce, and sugar to noodles and cook on high heat until the sugar has dissolved and the sauce is shimmering.&#10;4.  Add the noodles to the pan, toss to coat in the sauce, and cook until just heated through. &#10;5.  Can top with more fried scallions from scallion oil if preferred.           |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Pad See Ew\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ohy1DELF4is) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 170 g (6 oz) Beef or protein of choice, thinly sliced (6-7 slices of costco thinly sliced beef)&#10;  - 1 lb (450 g) Ho Fun Noodles (Fresh wide rice noodles)&#10;  - 4 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 2 Eggs&#10;  - 7 Stalks Chinese Broccoli, thinly sliced\*\*For sauce:\*\*  - 5 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Oyster Sauce&#10;  - 2  Tbsp Light  Soy Sauce&#10;  - 1 ½ Tsp Fish Sauce&#10;  - 4 Tsp Dark Soy sauce&#10;  - 4 Tsp Vinegar&#10;  - 1 Tsp White pepper      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Combine sauce ingredients and stir to mix.&#10;2.  Heat pan with oil on high heat, add garlic and cook for 15 seconds. Add protein and cook until mostly cooked. &#10;3.  Add Chinese broccoli stems and cook until protein is cooked through&#10;4.  Add Chinese broccoli leaves and cook until just wilted.&#10;5.  Push everything to one side and crack eggs and scramble.&#10;6.  Remove everything and set aside.&#10;7.  Clean the pan with a paper towel to remove excess liquid. &#10;8.  Heat the pan with oil on high heat until smoking hot.&#10;9.  Add Ho Fun noodles and sauce..&#10;10. Toss as few times as possible to disperse sauce and make edges of noodles caramelized. About 1-1½ minutes.&#10;11. Quickly add protein and vegetables  back in and toss to disperse. &#10;12. Serve immediately.  ?Try spice? Gochujang? gochugaru?      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Pancit Bihon Guisado\*\*By \[Kuya Fern\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=025q17zw7JI) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 500g Bihon noodles&#10;  - 2 Medium Carrots, sliced into strips&#10;  - 2 Onions, chopped into strips&#10;  - 1 Garlic head, minced&#10;  - 600g Chicken thigh or breast, chopped into small pieces&#10;  - 1 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1 Tsp Black pepper&#10;  - 6 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - Optional: 4 Piece Chicken liver, sliced into small pieces&#10;  - Cabbage, chopped        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a wok, lightly toast carrots on medium heat with oil. Then set aside.&#10;2.  Saute chopped onions on high for about 20 seconds.&#10;3.  Add garlic and saute for another 10 seconds.&#10;4.  Add chicken and let fat render.&#10;5.  Add salt, black pepper, oyster sauce, and soy sauce. Mix and reduce on high heat.&#10;6.  Add chicken liver to add extra flavor (Optional). Continue to reduce.&#10;7.  Add 7 cups of hot water.&#10;8.  Mix and slow cook for about 5 minutes.&#10;9.  Separate/strain meat from broth. Set aside meat.&#10;10. Add bihon noodles and cook on low to medium to let noodles absorb flavors of broth.&#10;11. Can set some broth aside in case it is too much.&#10;12. Cook until broth is completely absorbed by noodles.&#10;13. Can readd broth if needed.&#10;14. Add carrots, chicken, and chopped cabbage. Turn off heat and mix thoroughly.         |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Radish Rice with Korean Sauce\*\*By \[Maangchi\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VV4wAJadyLA) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Korean Radish, cut into matchsticks&#10;  - 2 Cups White rice, washed\*\*For sauce:\*\*  - 4 Stalks Green onion, minced&#10;  - 4 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - ¼ Cup Soy sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sesame seeds&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Gochugaru      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Place half of the radish into the rice cooker.&#10;2.  Add rice, then top with the rest of the radish.&#10;3.  Add 1 cup of water, and start the rice cooker.&#10;4.  Serve rice with some sauce mixed in. \*\*Notes\*\*Sauce can be served with regular rice as well.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Spanish Rice\*\*By \[ArnieTex\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7kSiLTnKw0I) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Tbsp oil&#10;  - ¾ Cup rice, uncooked&#10;  - ⅓ Cup Tomato sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Garlic powder&#10;  - 1 Tsp Onion powder&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Chicken bouillon&#10;  - 1 Tsp salt, to taste&#10;  - Optional: 1 Pinch dried Oregano&#10;  - 2 Cups Chicken broth      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat oil in a pot or dutch oven. Add rice and mix. Continue to stir until browned evenly.&#10;2.  Add tomato sauce and Spice mix.&#10;3.  Add chicken broth and ½ cup water, then mix.&#10;4.  Lower heat to a simmer and cover for about 10-15 minutes. Ensure that rice does not burn or dry out.&#10;5.  Turn off heat when just a little liquid is left. Leave lid on and allow pot to sit to continue cooking after the heat is turned off. Rice should be nice and fluffy when ready, not sticky. Mix when ready. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with \[Carnitas and Salsa\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.8rlfbmzgmv2v) or \[Al Pastor Tacos\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.boc2i89jf2fv).      |

Can serve on \[Baked Buffalo Chicken Wings\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.6zq2p3bobrr2).         |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Stir Fry Sauce\*\*By \[RecipeTinEats\](https://www.recipetineats.com/real-chinese-purpose-stir-fry-sauce/) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 0.5 Cup Light Soy Sauce0.5 Cup Oyster Sauce  - 0.25 Cup Chinese wine or Dry Sherry&#10;  - 0.25 Cup Corn starch&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 1 Tsp to 1 Tbsp White pepper, per preference        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Mix ingredients in a jar and shake to combine. Store in refrigerator and shake before use.&#10;2.  Heat 2 Tbsp oil on high heat in pan.&#10;3.  Add base flavoring of choice (garlic, ginger, dried chili peppers, shallots) and fry for 10 seconds.&#10;4.  Add stir fry ingredients in order of time to cook (starting with ingredients that take longest to cook), leaving leafy greens, like leaves of bok choy, until when you add the sauce (otherwise they will wilt and overcook).&#10;5.  Add noodles (if using), sauce and water (amounts below), choice of additional flavorings and any leafy greens.&#10;6.  Gently toss to combine and let sauce cook for around 1 minutes. Sauce will become thick and glossy and coats stir fry.&#10;7.  Serve over rice.\*\*  &#10;\*\*\*\*Amount to Use\*\*  - \*\*\\\*\\\*Stir Fry:\\\*\\\*\*\*   &#10;      - 3 Tbsp Stir Fry Sauce + 6 Tbsp Water + 5 Cups Uncooked ingredients&#10;  - \*\*\\\*\\\*Noodles:\\\*\\\*\*\*  &#10;      - 3 Tbsp Stir Fry Sauce + 6 Tbsp Water + 7 Cups Uncooked ingredients&#10;  - \*\*\\\*\\\*By Weight:\\\*\\\*\*\*  &#10;      - 3 Tbsp Stir Fry Sauce per 1 lb/500 g Uncooked ingredients  |

Add ingredients to pan and cook down to reduce into a syrup.&#10;2. | \#\#\#\# \*\*Salsa\*\*  |  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 12 Tomatillos or 6 Roma tomatoes&#10;  - 1-3 Jalapeno peppers, sliced in half&#10;      &#10;      - Alternatives: Serrano or anaheim pepper&#10;  - 3-5 Garlic cloves&#10;  - ½-1 Onion, sliced into quarters&#10;  - 8-10 Sticks Cilantro&#10;  - Big pinch Salt&#10;  - 1 Lime    |   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Peel tomatillos (Do not peel if using roma tomatoes) and slice in half. Place them face down onto another metal sheet.&#10;2.  Cut jalapenos in half and place face down onto the  metal sheet. May remove seeds and stem for less spicy. Can add more peppers for more spice. &#10;3.  Smash 3-5 garlic cloves and add to the sheet.&#10;4.  Set the oven to broil and place the metal sheet with the tomatillos, jalapeno, onions, and garlic into the oven until charred. Then transfer to a blender.&#10;5.  In the blender, also add cilantro, salt, and juice from 1 lime. Blend roughly and ready serve. May adjust salt and lime as needed. \*\*Notes\*\*  |
