| \#\#\#\# \*\*Cantonese Chili Oil\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=omuoTY3T16w) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*  \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1.5 Oz Dried chili flakes, or crushed dried chili peppers (Sichuan, or preference)&#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  - ½ Tsp Sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp White pepper&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Paprika for red color&#10;  - 1-2 Ginger nubs, thinly sliced&#10;  - ½ Red onion, sliced into thin strips&#10;  - 3-4 Garlic cloves, roughly minced&#10;  - 2 Star anise, roughly smashed and wet with water&#10;  - Optional: Shallots&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sesame oil.     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Mix chilis, salt, sugar, white pepper, paprika and 1 Tbsp oil into a bowl.&#10;2.  Heat pan on high, then add 4 oz vegetable or corn oil.&#10;3.  In order, add ginger, garlic, shallots, then onion, star anise. Cook long enough until aromatic. Cooking for about 3-4 minutes.&#10;4.  Remove ingredients from the oil. &#10;5.  Reheat oil, keep around 300 degrees F. Can add room temp oil if too hot.&#10;6.  Pour 1 ladle of hot oil onto mixture and mix. Do not add all oil at once to avoid burning.&#10;7.  Sprinkle in some sesame seeds.&#10;8.  Add one ladle of hot oil.&#10;9.  Add 1 Tsp Sesame oil. \*\*Notes\*\*For spicer or different oils, can try different chilis.Lasts several months in the refrigerator.           |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Chimichurri Sauce\*\*By \[Regester For Dinner\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PRxNQZaoSAo) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Bunches of Flat Leaf Italian Parsley, stems chopped off, minced&#10;  - ¼ Cup Oregano, fresh (minced) or dried&#10;  - 5-6 Garlic cloves, minced or pressed&#10;  - 1 Cup Olive oil&#10;  - 1 Cup Apple cider vinegar or Red wine vinegar&#10;  - 2 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 15-20 cranks Black Pepper&#10;  - Optional: Crushed red chili flakes       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  If using dried oregano, steep it in a cup of warm water for 3-5 minutes&#10;2.  Combine all ingredients in a mixing bowl.&#10;3.  Let sit overnight before serving. \*\*Notes\*\*Could serve with steak, ribs, chicken, burgers.         |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Crispy Garlic and Garlic Oil\*\*By \[Spoon Fork Heart\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=v3ZGTG9JDXo\&t=186s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Garlic head, minced&#10;  - 1 Cups Oil       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Cook garlic on low heat.&#10;2.  After about 6 minutes, garlic should turn golden brown.&#10;3.  Turn off heat.&#10;4.  Sift oil from garlic.&#10;5.  Let garlic cool before storing in a container to allow it to stay crispy. \*\*Notes\*\*Could serve with \[Maple Fish Sauce Brussels Sprouts\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.5k7qc0jtqp78)         |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Ginger Scallion Sauce\*\*By \[Made With Lau\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mIaAMIUd6WQ) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 4 Scallion/Green onion stalks, minced&#10;  - 3 Ginger nubs, minced&#10;  - 6 Tbsp Grapeseed or corn oil&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 2 Tsp salt       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat oil in a pan on high until shimmering or ripples form.&#10;2.  Add ginger and cook for 15 seconds.&#10;3.  Add scallions and cook for 20-30 seconds.&#10;4.  Transfer sauce to a bowl.&#10;5.  Add sesame oil and salt, then mix. \*\*Notes\*\*Could add soy sauce or oyster sauce.Amount of scallions, ginger, and oil can vary based on preference.Sesame oil is important for the taste. Can serve with \[Poached Chicken\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.7f9mrcqyqvwr).         |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Katsu Sauce\*\*By \[Aaron and Claire\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZfF7yTbc4fY\&t=214s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 ½ Cups \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 6 Tbsp Ketchup&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Worcestershire sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Mix ingredients to serve. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with \[Chicken Cutlet\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.5gpnfd152n4d).         |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Nam Jim Jaew\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EVeIrzR05Vs) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Tamarind paste/concentrate&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Lime juice&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Palm or White sugar&#10;  - Shallots&#10;  - Cilantro&#10;  - Green onions&#10;  - Toasted chili powder or flakes&#10;  - Rice        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Toast uncook rice on a dry pan until dark brown. Grind in mortar and pestle. \*\*Notes\*\*  - Dipping sauce for meat.&#10;  - For fatty meats or sweet marinated meat, or thin meats, can make a lighter recipe to adjust ratio. Less sauce will cling to meat:&#10;      &#10;      - 1 Tbsp Tamarind&#10;      - 1.5 Tbsp Lime juice&#10;      - 2 Tbsp Fish sauce&#10;      - 1/3 Tbsp Sugar             |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Prik Nam Pla\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=isLl3m\_JUx4) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 7-8 Thai Chilis, diced&#10;  - ⅓ Cup Fish sauce&#10;  - 1 ½  Limes, juice squeezed&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sugar, dissolved in 1 tsp warm water&#10;  - Optional: Thinly sliced garlic and/or shallots        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add all ingredients together. Store in the refrigerator and it should last a long time.&#10;2.     \*\*Notes\*\*  - Can start with a ratio of 3:1 Fish sauce to lime juice and adjust to preference.&#10;  - For more mild spice, can chop chillies into bigger pieces, remove seeds/pith, or add chopped chillies to cold water first and let sit for a few minutes.&#10;  - Can use on plain/fried rice, fried eggs,  pan seared fish/chicken, or, mashed potatoes.&#10;  - Best fish sauce brands: Squid, Megachef, or Red Boat. Red Boat is a premium brand with more intense fish flavor, and can add sugar to help balance the fish flavor.             |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Shanghai-Style Scallion Oil\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez-Alt\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=lC87cKrwU5I) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 ½ Cups \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Cups Neutral oil (Rice bran, peanut, vegetable, canola, lard, grapeseed)&#10;  - 8 Scallion/Green onion stalks, cut into 2-inch segments&#10;  - Optional: 2 Medium Shallots, thinly sliced&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: Onions         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Place cut vegetables into pan and add oil.&#10;2.  High heat until oil starts to bubble gently, then reduce to medium for a slow, lazy bubble. Cook for about 10-15 minutes while stirring to cook evenly.&#10;3.  Remove from the heat and can store in the refrigerator for up to 2 months. \*\*Notes\*\*Can use in \[Shanghai-style Noodles\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.glry5t9ztt00).Can serve with \[Poached Chicken\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.7f9mrcqyqvwr).         |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*\\\*Stir Fry Sauce\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Garlic cloves, minced or pressed&#10;  - 1 Stalk Green onion, chopped&#10;  - 1 Ginger nub&#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  - 1 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tsp Cornstarch + 1 ½ Tbsp Water&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Cooking wine&#10;  - ½ Tsp White pepper&#10;  - 1 Tsp Fish sauce&#10;  - Dash of Sesame oil        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat a neutral oil on medium. Add garlic, green onions, and ginger until aromatic.&#10;2.  Add the rest of the ingredients except for sesame oil.&#10;3.  Add vegetables for stir frying&#10;4.  At the end of cooking, add a dash of sesame oil. \*\*Notes\*\*Can cook with \[Boiled Mushrooms\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.zbp8a6gc8zu0).          |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Sukang Sawsawan (Spiced Vinegar)\*\*By \[TasteGie\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=g01QvSqkklw\&t=1s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ⅓-½ Red onion, finely chopped&#10;  - 5-7 Garlic cloves, minced&#10;  - Optional: 3 Chili peppers, chopped&#10;  - ½ Tbsp ginger, minced&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Kosher salt&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp ground pepper&#10;  - 1 ¼ Cup Distilled white vinegar&#10;  - ½ Tsp Soy sauce        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Mix all ingredients into a jar and let rest for 30-60 minutes before serving. \*\*Notes\*\*Can add more soy sauce for a darker color.Can substitute onion with cucumber for a milder taste.Can adjust sugar and salt according to taste. Can be paired with grilled or fried dishes, including lumpiang shanghai (spring rolls), vegetable fritters (okoy), barbecue, crispy pata, pork chops, kwek-kwek (quail eggs). Can serve with \[Pork Tocino\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.pu97mhjcjizt).          |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Thai Basil Sauce\*\*By \[Pailin’s Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=T6tYHmgVnrk) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Fish sauce&#10;  - 2 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Small onion, chopped&#10;  - 6 Cloves Garlic, minced&#10;  - 2 Thai chilis, chopped&#10;  - ¾ Cup Stock, unsalted&#10;  - ½ Tsp Dark soy sauce&#10;  - ½ Cup Bell pepper, chopped&#10;  - 1.5 Cup Thai basil or any basil   | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To a small bowl, add soy sauce, oyster sauce, fish sauce, and sugar&#10;2.  To a medium heat pan, add oil. Add onions and saute to preference.&#10;3.  Add garlic and thai chilis.&#10;4.  Once garlic is starting to turn golden, add stock and sauce mixture. Stir.&#10;5.  Add black or white pepper to taste.&#10;6.  Add dark soy sauce and bell peppers.&#10;7.  Turn off heat and add basil. Use residual heat to wilt the basil.&#10;8.  Taste to ensure not too salty. Can add more stock or water.&#10;9.  Can be used as a pour over sauce or stir fry sauce. Heat up the sauce and ladle over a cooked protein. As stir fry, sear protein, stir fry until 80% cooked, add vegetables, Add sauce and stir fry. \*\*Notes\*\*  - Will keep in the fridge for at least a week.           |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Bean Paste Stew\*\*By |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Tbsp Cooking oil&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Bean paste&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Ssamjang (Seasoned bean paste)&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Gochujang&#10;  - ¼ Onion, sliced&#10;  - ½ Tofu package, sliced into rectangles&#10;  - Enoki mushrooms&#10;  - 1-2 Green onion stalks, minced&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Anchovy seasoning or Fish sauce&#10;  - 3-4 piece thinly sliced beef brisket      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  On medium heat, add cooking  oil to pot.&#10;2.  Add bean paste, ssamjang, and gochujang. Mix.&#10;3.  Add 2 cups of water.&#10;4.  Add vegetables and beef. Cook until ready. \*\*Notes\*\*           |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Miso Soup\*\*By \[Kenji Lopez-Alt\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=51GpBG4iJcA) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 4 Cups Water&#10;  - 1  Tbsp Miso paste&#10;  - If Miso paste does not contain dashi:&#10;      &#10;      - 1 sheet kombu dashi or powder dashi&#10;  - ½ Tbsp Wakame seaweed, dried&#10;  - ½ Package cubed tofu (Silken or Firm)&#10;  - ½ Cup Scallions, chopped         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Place water and Kombu into pot bringing to a boil.&#10;2.  Bring heat down to medium and add miso paste and stir until dissolved.&#10;3.  Stir in dried wakame and tofu and cook for a couple more minutes.&#10;4.  Serve with scallions on top. \*\*Notes\*\*Can serve with \[Miso Glazed Salmon\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.srtio735x1ke)Optional: Could add vegetables and kimchi.          |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Ramen (Soy Sauce Soup)\*\*By \[Kimono Mom\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iR8fXITKqAE) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies on amount of pork \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Tsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - 1 Tsp Chicken broth&#10;  - 1 Tsp Oyster sauce&#10;  - ½ Tsp Sesame oil&#10;  - 1 Clove Garlic, grated&#10;  - 350 mL Water(1.5 Cups)&#10;  - Salt&#10;  - Pepper&#10;  - Ramen noodles.&#10;  - \[Chashu\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.uv1hdjjbo46i)&#10;  - \[Seasoned  eggs\](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A9Di8ywaO38nGCVOqGwirgZopJRu\_i\_r6AMFC8XHNUY/edit\#heading=h.4acdwnwjnvc7)        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Prepare Chashu and seasoned eggs ahead of time.&#10;2.  Add sugar, soy sauce, chicken broth, oyster sauce, sesame oil, garlic, salt, and pepper. Mix and set aside.&#10;3.  Prepare ramen noodles per package instructions.&#10;4.  Add 1.5 Cups hot water to the broth mixture. Add cooked noodles.&#10;5.  Can double the recipe for 2 people.&#10;6.  Top with chashu, eggs, nori, and green onions.      |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Stock (Chicken/Vegetable)\*\*By Sandy Clement |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*2 Servings \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - Large zip lock bag full of frozen veggie scraps that you've saved over time. Celery tops & bottom, carrots, onion including skins (not red), etc. Basically any part you normally throw out goes in bag.&#10;  - Large zip lock bag full of frozen chicken carcasses, bones, fat, skin, etc. that you've saved over time.&#10;  - 2 large bay leaves or equilivent small leaves&#10;  - 1 Tbs poultry seasoning&#10;  - 1 tsp salt&#10;  - ½ tsp pepper         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Place ingredients in large mesh basket inside large stock pot. Cover with water. Simmer on back burner for 3-4 hours or until flavors come up. Season as required.&#10;2.  Remove mesh basket containing cooked veggies & bones and place in bowl to drain. Be careful not to splash hot broth. When contents of basket have cooled, discard. Return any drained broth to stock pot.&#10;3.  Place in appropriate containers in 1 cup or 2 cup increments for future cooking needs and Freeze.  \*\*Notes\*\*To make chicken pho broth, add sugar, salt, fish sauce, star anise, cinnamon. Serve with chicken, rice noodles, and green onions.          |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Chocolate Chip Cookies\*\*By \[RV Manabat\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sJsXDi26b-Y) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ¼ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - ½ Cup Brown sugar&#10;  - ½ Cup White sugar&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 ¼ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - ½ Cup Chocolate chips or White Chocolate chips        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add butter, brown sugar, and white sugar to a mixing bowl. Mix until fully mixed, but do not over mix.&#10;2.  Add egg and vanilla extract and mix until very smooth (emulsified).&#10;3.  In another mixing bowl, add flour, baking soda, and chocolate chips. Add salt if using unsalted butter. Mix until combined.&#10;4.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until consistency is not too hard or too soft. Mix until no traces of flour are seen. This mixture can last up to 1 week inside the refrigerator, or can be frozen. &#10;5.  Scoop batter onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;6.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 12-14 minutes.&#10;7.  Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.             |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Cream Cheese Cookies\*\*By \[Preppy Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4TEVFbCpsME\&t=307s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ¼ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - 4 Oz or 113 g Cream cheese, room temperature&#10;  - 1 Cup White sugar&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - 2 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 ¾ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - Powdered sugar        | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add butter, cream cheese, and white sugar to a mixing bowl. Mix until fully mixed, but do not over mix.&#10;2.  Add egg and vanilla extract and mix until very smooth (emulsified).&#10;3.  In another mixing bowl add flour and baking soda. Add salt if using unsalted butter. Mix until combined.&#10;4.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until consistency is not too hard or too soft. Mix until no traces of flour are seen. &#10;5.  Refrigerate mixture for at least 1 hour. This mixture can last up to 1 week inside the refrigerator, or can be frozen. &#10;6.  Scoop batter onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;7.  Cook at 375 degrees F for 10-11 minutes.&#10;8.  Place on the cooling rack.&#10;9.  Top with a little bit of powdered sugar.             |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Oreo Cookies\*\*Modified recipe by Monica M. |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ¼ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - 6 Tbsp Brown sugar&#10;  - 6 Tbsp White sugar&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 ¼ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - 11 Oreo cookies, broken into pieces      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\. Scoop batter onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;7.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 12-14 minutes.&#10;8.  Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.             |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Gingerbread Latte Cookies\*\*By \[Vaughn Vreeland\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vT0batrV3jE\&t=118s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ¾ Cup (12 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ¾ Cup Unsalted butter, and add 1 Tsp Kosher salt &#10;  - ¼ Cup Espresso powder&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Ginger, grated&#10;  - 2 Tsp Ground Ginger&#10;  - 1 Tsp Ground Cinnamon&#10;  - ½ Tsp Ground Nutmeg&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Ground Clove&#10;  - ½ Cup Brown sugar, packed&#10;  - ½ Cup White sugar&#10;  - ¼ Cup Unsulphured Molasses&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - 2 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - 2 Cups All-purpose flour, sifted\*\*For coating:\*\*  - ¼ Cup White sugar&#10;  - 2 Tsp Espresso powder&#10;  - ½ Tsp Ground Ginger | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Melt butter in a pot. Remove from heat and add ground ginger, nutmeg, clove, cinnamon, espresso powder, and fresh ginger. Mix and let sit for 5 minutes to cool slightly. &#10;2.  In a medium mixing bowl, add brown sugar, white sugar, and molasses. Add butter mixture to bowl and whisk.&#10;3.  Add egg and whisk until smooth and homogenous. Add vanilla extract and baking soda. Mix&#10;4.  Add flour and mix. Chill for at least 2 hours.&#10;5.  Scoop batter and roll scoop in coating mixture to coat. Then place the scoop  onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;6.  Cook at 375 degrees F for 10  minutes.&#10;7.  Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.           |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Gochujang Caramel Cookies\*\*By \[Eric Kim\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4B43W\_wXJuY\&t=330s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ¼ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Brown sugar, packed&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Gochujang&#10;  - 1 Cup White sugar&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 ½ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Cinnamon&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda       | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  In a small bowl, add brown sugar, gochujang, and 1 Tbsp butter. Mix together to form a paste.&#10;2.  In another mixing bowl, add the rest of the butter and white sugar. Mix until fully mixed, but do not over mix.&#10;3.  Add egg and vanilla extract and mix until very smooth (emulsified).&#10;4.  In another mixing bowl, add flour, baking soda, and cinnamon. Add salt if using unsalted butter. Mix until combined.&#10;5.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until consistency is not too hard or too soft. Mix until no traces of flour are seen. Can refrigerate until less sticky, 15-20 minutes.&#10;6.  In 3-4 spaced out blobs, add the gochujang paste over the cookie dough. Switch mixture in circular strokes to get streaks throughout. Do not overmix to get distinct strips of gochujang.&#10;7.  Scoop batter onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;8.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 12-14 minutes.&#10;9.  Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.           |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Lemon Cookies\*\*By \[Preppy Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VRLsDE1LTUw\&t=418s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ½ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - ½ Cup White sugar&#10;  - ½ Cup Brown sugar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Lemon zest (2 lemons)&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Lemon juice&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 2 Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - 2 Tbsp cornstarch&#10;  - 1 Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - Powdered sugar  | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To a bowl, add sugar, lemon zest. And work zest into the sugar.&#10;2.  Add butter and sugar.&#10;3.  Add eggs and lemon juice. Add 1 Tbsp of lemon extract or lemon paste.&#10;4.  In another mixing bowl, add flour, cornstarch and baking soda. Add salt if using unsalted butter. Mix until combined.&#10;5.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until no traces of flour are seen.&#10;6.  Could add food coloring or turmeric to adjust color.&#10;7.  Wrap and refrigerate for at least 30 minutes.&#10;8.  Scoop batter and place onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;9.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 10 minutes.&#10;10. Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.&#10;11. To make glaze. Take 1 Cup of powdered sugar, 2 Tbsp lemon juice, whisk until glaze consistency. Drizzle over cookies.           |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*?Lemon Crinkle Cookies\*\*By \[Preppy Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fsiJrbLR3kg) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ½ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - 1 Cup White sugar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Lemon zest (2 lemons)&#10;  - ¼ Cup Lemon juice&#10;  - 2 Large eggs&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 2 ⅓ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - Powdered sugar      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To a bowl, add sugar, lemon zest. And work zest into the sugar.&#10;2.  Add eggs to sugar add melted butter.&#10;3.  Add lemon juice.&#10;4.  Add vanilla extract and mix until very smooth (emulsified).&#10;5.  In another mixing bowl, add flour and baking soda. Add salt if using unsalted butter. Mix until combined.&#10;6.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until no traces of flour are seen.&#10;7.  Could add food coloring or turmeric to adjust color.&#10;8.  Wrap and refrigerate for at least 4 hours.&#10;9.  Add powdered sugar to a bowl.&#10;10. Scoop batter into white sugar, then powdered sugar, then place onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;11. Cook at 350 degrees F for 12-14 minutes.&#10;12. Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.            |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Matcha White Chocolate Chip Cookies\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ¼ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - ½ Cup Brown sugar&#10;  - ½ Cup White sugar&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 ¼ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - ½-1 Tbsp Matcha powder&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - ½ White Chocolate chips      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add butter, brown sugar, and white sugar to a mixing bowl. Mix until fully mixed, but do not over mix.&#10;2.  Add egg and vanilla extract and mix until very smooth (emulsified).&#10;3.  In another mixing bowl, add flour, matcha powder, baking soda, and white chocolate chips. Add salt if using unsalted butter. Mix until combined.&#10;4.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until consistency is not too hard or too soft. Mix until no traces of flour are seen. This mixture can last up to 1 week inside the refrigerator, or can be frozen. &#10;5.  Scoop batter onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;6.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 12-14 minutes.&#10;7.  Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.             |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Molasses Cookies\*\*By Claire Saffits |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ¼ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - 1 ½ Sticks(6 oz/170g) Melted unsalted butter, cooled to room temp&#10;  - ½ Cup Regular molasses&#10;  - 2 Tsp Apple cider vinegar&#10;  - ½ Cup Dark Brown sugar&#10;  - 2 Large eggs, room temp&#10;  - Optional: 2 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 3 ¼ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Baking soda&#10;  - ¼ Tsp Ground Clove&#10;  - 2 ½ Tsp Ground Ginger&#10;  - ½ Tsp finely Ground Black pepper&#10;  - ½ Tsp Allspice&#10;  - ½ Tsp Kosher Salt&#10;  - ½ Cup Granulated sugar | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add butter, brown sugar until mixture is smooth, but not fluffy.&#10;2.  Add eggs and molasses, apple cider vinegar, vanilla extract and mix until very smooth (emulsified).&#10;3.  In another mixing bowl, add flour, baking soda, clove, ginger, all spice, black pepper, and salt.&#10;4.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until consistency is not too hard or too soft. Mix until no traces of flour are seen. This mixture can last up to 1 week inside the refrigerator, or can be frozen. &#10;5.  Dough should be soft and sticky. Refrigerate for at least an hour. Up to 2 days. Can freeze dough.&#10;6.  Roll dough into 2 Tbsp balls. Roll between palms to form and coat in granulated sugar. Place onto a baking sheet.&#10;7.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 12-14 minutes.&#10;8.  Cool on baking sheet for 10 minutes.&#10;9.  Cookies can be stored air tight container for 5 days.              |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Nutella Cookies\*\*By \[Preppy Kitchen\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=C1OgkRI7VdE) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Cup Nutella&#10;  - 1 egg&#10;  - ¾ Cup Flour      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add nutella and egg to a mixing bowl. Mix until smooth.&#10;2.  Add flour and mix until just combined. Do not overmix.&#10;3.  Scoop batter onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;4.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 10-12  minutes.&#10;5.  Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.             |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Sesame Miso Cookies\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ¼ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - ½ Cup Brown sugar&#10;  - ½ Cup White sugar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Miso&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 ¼ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - Toasted Sesame seeds      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add butter, miso, brown sugar, and white sugar to a mixing bowl. Mix until fully mixed, but do not over mix.&#10;2.  Add egg and vanilla extract and mix until very smooth (emulsified).&#10;3.  In another mixing bowl, add flour and baking soda. Add salt if using unsalted butter. Mix until combined.&#10;4.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until consistency is not too hard or too soft. Mix until no traces of flour are seen.&#10;5.  Scoop batter and roll scoop in sesame seeds to coat. Flatten scoops a little. Then place the scoop  onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;6.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 12-14 minutes.&#10;7.  Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.             |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Snickerdoodle Cookies\*\*  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - ½ Cup (8 Tbsp) Salted butter, room temperature&#10;      &#10;      - Alternative: ½ Cup Unsalted butter, and add ¼ Tsp iodized salt added to flour mixture&#10;  - ½ Cup Brown sugar&#10;  - ½ Cup White sugar&#10;  - 1 Large egg&#10;  - Optional: 1 Tsp Vanilla extract&#10;  - 1 ¼ Cups All-purpose flour, sifted&#10;  - ½ Tsp Baking soda&#10;  - ¾ Tsp Cinnamon\*\*For sugar coating ratio:\*\*  - 1 Tbsp White sugar&#10;  - ½ Tsp Cinnamon      | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add butter, brown sugar, and white sugar to a mixing bowl. Mix until fully mixed, but do not over mix.&#10;2.  Add egg and vanilla extract and mix until very smooth (emulsified).&#10;3.  In another mixing bowl, add flour, baking soda, and ¾ Tsp cinnamon. Add salt if using unsalted butter. Mix until combined.&#10;4.  Combine both mixtures. Mix until consistency is not too hard or too soft. Mix until no traces of flour are seen. This mixture can last up to 1 week inside the refrigerator, or can be frozen. &#10;5.  Scoop batter and roll scoop in sugar coating (white sugar mixed with cinnamon) to coat. Then place the scoop  onto a baking pan with parchment paper or silicone baking mat.&#10;6.  Cook at 350 degrees F for 12-14 minutes.&#10;7.  Place on cooling rack until ready to serve.             |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Caramel\*\*By  |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Cup Heavy cream&#10;  - 5 Tbsp Unsalted Butter&#10;  - ½ Tsp Salt&#10;  -   &#10;  - ¼ Cup Light Corn syrup&#10;  - ¼ Cup Water&#10;  - 1 ⅓ Cup sugar &#10;  -   &#10;  - 1 ½ Cups Sugar&#10;  - ¼ Cup Water&#10;  -     | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Add ingredients and bring to boil over medium heat. Then turn off and cover for 10 minutes.&#10;2.  Line pan with parchment, spray with vegetable oil.&#10;3.  Add corn syrup, water, and sugar to a pan. Heat over medium high heat until sugar dissolves. Do not stir. Cook until it registers 300 degrees. 7-9 minutes. Lower heat to medium low, then gently swirl as you continue to cook for 2-3 minutes.&#10;4.  Wait until the color is amber, once mixture is 350 degrees it is done.&#10;5.  Remove saucepan from heat, and stir in cream mixture. Cook until it reaches 248 degrees.&#10;6.  Stir so it doesn't burn, should take about 5 minutes.&#10;7.  Pour into the pan, let cool for 10 minutes and add salt if preferred.&#10;8.  Cool for 1 hour at room temp, then in the fridge for another hour. &#10;9.  Cut into preferred size. Will keep for up to 2 weeks in refrigerator Chocolate:Finely chop chocolate. Put microwave for 30 seconds. Stir. Another 30 seconds, then 15 seconds. Check to make sure it does not go over 90 degrees. Place caramel onto Nilla wafers, then cover top with melted chocolate.      |

| \#\#\#\# \*\*Caramel Bread Popcorn\*\*By \[Nino’s Home\](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mTR1ogC42F4\&t=208s) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 2-3 Slices of Bread, sliced into small squares&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Sugar&#10;  - 1 Tbsp Butter&#10;  - 3 Tbsp Non-sugar Milk         | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Heat breadcrumbs in pan until crispy. About 5-10 minutes. Set aside.&#10;2.  In a pan on medium heat, add sugar and 1 Tbsp water. Do not stir. Heat until golden, not golden brown.&#10;3.  Then add butter and milk. Stir constantly until brown.&#10;4.  Add bread to caramel.&#10;5.  Spread out to cool on parchment paper until crispy.&#10;6.  Serve with a cup of milk.             |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Cinnamon Rolls\*\*By Diana T. |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*\*\*For Starter:\*\*  - ⅓ Cup Bread flour&#10;  - ½ Cup Whole milk&#10;  - ½ Cup Water\*\*For Dough:\*\*  - 5 Cups Bread flour&#10;  - ½ Cup Sugar&#10;  - 4 Tsp Active dry yeast&#10;  - 2 Tsp Salt&#10;  - 2 Eggs&#10;  - 1 Cup Warm whole milk, plus extra for brushing on the unbaked loaf&#10;  - 8 Tbsp Unsalted butter, cut into pieces and softened at room temperature, plus extra for buttering bowls and pan\*\*For Filling:\*\*  - 1 Stick softened butter&#10;  - 1 Cup Brown sugar, packed&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Cinnamon&#10;  - ½ Cup Heavy cream, for pouring over risen rolls | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  To make the Starter, in a small heavy pot, whisk flour, milk and water together until smooth. Bring to a simmer over medium-low heat and cook, stirring often, until thickened but still pourable, about 10 minutes (it will thicken more as it cools). When it’s ready, the spoon will leave tracks on the bottom of the pot. Scrape into a measuring cup and lightly cover the surface with plastic wrap. Set aside to cool to room temperature.&#10;2.  To make the Dough, In the bowl of a stand mixer fitted with a dough hook, combine the flour, sugar, yeast and salt and mix for a few seconds, just until evenly combined.&#10;3.  Add egg, milk, and Starter to the Dough. Mix and knead for 5 minutes.&#10;4.  Add soft butter and knead another 10 to 12 minutes (it will take a few minutes for butter to be incorporated), until the dough is smooth and springy and just a bit tacky.&#10;5.  Lightly butter the inside of a bowl. Use your hands to lift dough out of mixer bowl, shape into a ball and place in prepared bowl. Cover with a kitchen towel and let rise in a warm place until doubled in size, 40 to 60 minutes.&#10;6.  After you let the dough rise in a greased covered bowl until it is double in size, you’re ready to roll the cinnamon rolls. Use a pastry mat sprinkled with flour to keep the dough from sticking. Roll out the dough into a rectangle about ¼ inch thick.&#10;7.  After you’ve rolled the dough, spread the cinnamon filling on top and roll-up jelly-roll style. Cut into 12 rolls for a 9×13 pan.  |

  

|  |  |
| :-: | :-: |
| \#\#\#\# \*\*Furikake Chex Mix\*\*By \[Monica M. \](https://www.hawaiimagazine.com/recipe-hawaii-style-furikake-chex-mix/) |  |
| \*\*Yield\*\*Varies \*\*Ingredients\*\*  - 1 Box Corn Chex Mix&#10;  - ½ Bag Pretzels&#10;  - ½ Bag Goldfish&#10;  - ½ Bag Bugles&#10;  - ½ Cup Butter&#10;  - ½ Cup Maple syrup&#10;  - ¼ Cup Vegetable oil&#10;  - ¼ Cup White sugar&#10;  - 2 Tbsp Soy sauce&#10;  - Umebashi furikake bottle          | \*\*Directions\*\*1\.  Preheat the oven to 250 degrees F. Add chex mix, pretzels, goldfish, and bugles onto 1-2 baking sheets.&#10;2.  In a sauce pot, melt butter and brown, meaning on low heat cook butter until a caramel color. Could take up to 10-15 minutes. Continue to stir.&#10;3.  Add sugar and stir.&#10;4.  Add maple syrup, vegetable oil, and soy sauce. Bring to a boil. Sugar should be dissolved and sauce should be sticky. Continue to stir until you see it coat your spatula. If it is super runny, it is not done yet. Careful not to burn.&#10;5.  Pour butter mixture over dry ingredient mix and toss and coat well. Sprinkle furikake over mix and stir to make sure it is fully covered. Bake for 1 hour, but toss every 15 minutes.             |
